function v27StartStep(){const {d,r,second,question,options,notes}=v27StepInfo();r.sequence=[];r.from='';r.to='';r.assisted=false;r.failed=false;r.done=false;$('v27Reveal').hidden=true;$('v27Reveal').innerHTML='';$('v27StepDone').hidden=true;$('v27Feedback').textContent=second?'New situation.  let html='';if(d.type==='piano')html=`<p class="v27-prompt">${h(second?'Now play this different pattern.':question)}</p><div class="v27-notes">${notes.map((n,j)=>`<span data-v27-pitch="${j}">${h(n)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="v27-piano-tools"><button type="button" id="v27Demo">▶ Hear the pattern</button><button type="button" id="v27RestartNotes">Restart attempt</button></div>${v27WhiteKeyboard()}<p class="v27-helptext">Use the keys to play the pattern in order. C4 is middle C; C3 is one octave below it. Each key makes a sound.</p>`;
 else if(d.type==='chess-board'&&!second)html=`<p class="v27-prompt">${h(d.question)}</p>${v27Board(d.fen)}<p class="v27-helptext">${d.square?'Tap the target square.':'Tap the piece, then its destination.'} White is at the bottom.</p>`;
 else if(d.type==='choice'||(d.type==='chess-board'&&second))html=`<p class="v27-prompt">${h(question)}</p><div class="v27-options">${options.map((op,j)=>`<button type="button" data-v27-choice="${j}">${h(op)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;
 else html=`<label for="v27Typed" class="v27-prompt">${h(question)}</label><div class="v27-typed"><input type="text" id="v27Typed" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="${d.type==='number'?'Enter a number':'Type what you would say'}"><button id="v27Check" type="button">Check answer</button></div><p class="v27-helptext">${d.type==='number'?'Use the requested units in the prompt; enter just the number.':'Different natural translations exist. Offline checking recognizes the model and common alternatives; an unrecognized answer may still be valid.'}</p>`;
 $('v27Activity').innerHTML=html;

 else if(d.type==='choice'||(d.type==='chess-board'&&second))$('v27Activity').querySelectorAll('[data-v27-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{if(r.done)return;const chosen=+b.dataset.v27Choice;v27Submit(chosen===+v27StepInfo().correct,options[chosen]);if(r.done)b.classList.add('right');else b.classList.add('wrong')});
 else{$('v27Check').onclick=()=>v27CheckTyped();$('v27Typed').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')v27CheckTyped()};}

function v27CheckTyped(){const {d,second,answer}=v27StepInfo(),val=$('v27Typed')?.value||'';if(!val.trim())return;if(d.type==='number'){const raw=val.trim().replace(/,/g,'');const actual=Number(raw),wanted=Number(second?d.secondAnswer:d.answer);v27Submit(Number.isFinite(actual)&&Math.abs(actual-wanted)<=Math.max(0,Number(d.tolerance)||0.00001),val)}else{const accepted=[answer,...(second?d.secondVariants:d.variants||[])];v27Submit(v27Equal(val,accepted),val)}}
function v27PressNote(n){const {notes,r}=v27StepInfo();if(r.done)return;v27Sound(n);const expected=notes[r.sequence.length];if(n!==expected){r.failed=true;r.sequence=[];$('v27Feedback').textContent=`You played ${n}. /* Practice across the 20 verified verb paradigms already authored in V23. */
const V27_TENSE_ORDER=['present','past','imperfect','subjunctive','nearFuture'];
const V27_TENSE_META={present:['Present','What is true now, or normally happens','A present tense names a current state or a habit.'],past:['Completed past','What happened once and finished','The preterite is used for completed, bounded events.'],imperfect:['Habitual past','What used to happen','The imperfect describes repeated past events or background.'],subjunctive:['Hope or wish','What someone hopes, doubts, or wants','The present subjunctive follows many expressions of hope, doubt, or wish.'],nearFuture:['Near future','What someone is going to do','Conjugate ir and add a + the unchanged infinitive.']};
let v27VerbSelection={verb:0,tense:'present',person:'yo'},v27VerbAssisted=false,v27VerbFailed=false;
function v27VerbKey(vi,t,person){return 'v'+vi+':'+t+':'+person}
function v27VerbRecords(){return safeStore.get('v27:verb-progress',{})||{}}
function v27VerbAnswer(vi,t,person){const verb=V23_VERBS[vi];if(!verb)return '';if(t==='nearFuture')return(person==='yo'?'voy a ':'vas a ')+verb.verb;const suffix={yo:'Yo',tu:'Tu'}[person];const field={present:'present',past:'past',imperfect:'imperfect',subjunctive:'subj'}[t]+suffix;return verb[field]||''}
function v27VerbPrompt(){const {verb:vi,tense:t,person:who}=v27VerbSelection,verb=V23_VERBS[vi];return t==='subjunctive'?`After “Espero que”, what ${who==='tu'?'tú':'yo'} form of ${verb.verb} (${verb.meaning}) fits?`:t==='nearFuture'?`How do you say “${who==='tu'?'you are':'I am'} going to ${verb.meaning}” using ir a + infinitive?`:`What is the ${who==='tu'?'tú':'yo'} ${t==='past'?'preterite':t} form of ${verb.verb} (${verb.meaning})?`}
function v27VerbNext(){const progress=v27VerbRecords(),items=[];for(let vi=0;vi<V23_VERBS.length;vi++)for(const t of V27_TENSE_ORDER)for(const who of ['yo','tu'])items.push({verb:vi,tense:t,person:who});const due=items.find(item=>progress[v27VerbKey(item.verb,item.tense,item.person)]?.reviewAt&&progress[v27VerbKey(item.verb,item.tense,item.person)].reviewAt<=Date.now());return due||items.find(item=>!progress[v27VerbKey(item.verb,item.tense,item.person)]?.independent)||items[0]}
function v27VerbSetSelection(){const vi=$('v27VerbChoose')?.value,t=$('v27TenseChoose')?.value,who=$('v27PersonChoose')?.value;v27VerbSelection={verb:Math.max(0,Number(vi)||0),tense:t||'present',person:who||'yo'};v27VerbRenderQuestion()}

function v27VerbRenderQuestion(){const {verb:vi,tense:t,person:who}=v27VerbSelection,answer=v27VerbAnswer(vi,t,who),meta=V27_TENSE_META[t],prior=v27VerbRecords()[v27VerbKey(vi,t,who)];v27VerbAssisted=false;v27VerbFailed=false;$('v27VerbQuestion').innerHTML=`<div class="v27-step">${h(meta[0])} · ${h(who==='tu'?'tú':'yo')}</div><h2>${h(v27VerbPrompt())}</h2><div class="v27-typed"><input type="text" id="v27VerbInput" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Type the correct form"><button id="v27VerbCheck" type="button">Check</button></div><div class="v27-feedback" id="v27VerbFeedback" role="status">${prior?.independent?'Already solved independently. /* V28 persistent tutor: authored local guidance, transparent optional online AI endpoint. */
const V28_TOPICS={
 spanish:[
 {keys:['subjunctive','subjuntivo','llames','tenga','venga','espero que','quiero que'],title:'The subjunctive',simple:'The subjunctive expresses how someone feels about an action: a wish, doubt, request, or possibility. It is not just another past or present tense.',steps:['Start with a trigger such as “Espero que” (I hope that).','Look at WHO must perform the next action. For tú, hablar becomes hables; llamar becomes llames.','Put the pieces together: Espero que me llames. The pronoun me goes before llames.'],example:'Quiero que me llames mañana = I want you to call me tomorrow. Quiero que = I want that; me = me; llames = you call (subjunctive).',mistake:'After “quiero que” for tú, use “llames”, not “llamas”. After “no quiero que”, it is still “llames”.'},
 {keys:['preterite','preterito','pretérito','completed past','past tense','fui','llamé'],title:'The completed past (preterite)',simple:'Use the preterite for a completed event with boundaries: something happened and ended.',steps:['Ask “Am I describing a completed event?”','For regular -ar verbs, the yo ending is -é: llamar → llamé.','For common irregular verbs, learn the pattern: ir (to go) → fui (I went).'],example:'Ayer llamé a mi amigo = Yesterday I called my friend.',mistake:'Do not use the imperfect just because the action was in the past; choose based on what the sentence means.'},
 {keys:['imperfect','imperfecto','used to','habitual past','vivía','llovía'],title:'The imperfect past',simple:'The imperfect gives background, descriptions, or repeated habits in the past. It is the “used to / was doing” feeling.',steps:['Ask whether the action was an ongoing scene or repeated habit.','For -ar verbs, use -aba endings; for -er/-ir, use -ía endings (with important irregulars).','Contrast the background with a finished event if needed.'],example:'Vivía en Chicago cuando era pequeño = I lived/used to live in Chicago when I was little. Llovía cuando salí = It was raining when I left.',mistake:'Finished event: salí. Background: llovía. Both can appear in one sentence.'},
 {keys:['ser','estar','estoy','soy'],title:'Ser versus estar',simple:'Both can mean “to be,” but ser identifies or classifies; estar describes location, condition, or a current state.',steps:['Ask if you are identifying what someone or something is. If yes, try ser.','If you are giving location or condition, try estar.','Say the whole sentence, not just the verb.'],example:'Soy estudiante = I am a student. Estoy en casa = I am at home. Estoy cansado = I am tired.',mistake:'“Estoy en casa” for location, not “Soy en casa.” Personal identity is usually ser.'},
 {keys:['por','para'],title:'Por versus para',simple:'Para often points toward a destination, recipient, or purpose. const V28_STOP=new Set('i me my a an the to of on in is it this that did do does what how why please could would can should you your about for with and when explain tell from start help question answer again more like understand it'.split(' '));
let v28Messages=[],v28Busy=false,v28Config={endpoint:'',useOnline:false},v28LastContextKey='',v28Focus=null,v28StepCounter=0;
const v28El=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function v28Escape(s){return String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]))}
function v28Normalized(s){return String(s??'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9ñ\s]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
function v28Context(){let s=typeof subject==='string'?subject:'spanish',mode='overview',item=null,title=(SECTIONS[s]?.name||s),q='',model='',why='',hint='',extras='',round=0;
 try{
  if(v27Active&&V27_TRACKS[v27Active.subject]){
   s=v27Active.subject;item=V27_TRACKS[s][v27Active.index];mode='continued lesson';title=item.title;
   const d=v27StepInfo(),second=!!d.second;round=second?2:1;q=d.question||item.question;why=d.why||item.why||'';hint=item.clue||'';
   model=item.type==='piano'?d.notes.join(' → '):item.type==='chess-board'&&!second?(item.move?item.move.slice(0,2)+' → '+item.move.slice(2):item.square):((item.type==='choice'||(item.type==='chess-board'&&second))?d.options[d.correct]:d.answer)||'';
   extras=(item.context||'')+(d.options?' Options: '+d.options.join(' | '):'');
  }else if(v28El('v27VerbQuestion')&&!v28El('v27VerbQuestion').hidden){
   s='spanish';mode='verb studio';const x=v27VerbSelection,verb=V23_VERBS[x.verb];title=verb.verb+' · '+V27_TENSE_META[x.tense][0];q=v27VerbPrompt();model=v27VerbAnswer(x.verb,x.tense,x.person);why=V27_TENSE_META[x.tense][2];hint=V27_TENSE_META[x.tense][1];extras='Meaning: '+verb.meaning;
  }else if(v23Active){s=v23Active.subject;mode='intro guided lesson';const source=(V23_TRACKS?.[s]||[])[v23Active.index];item=source||null;title=source?.title||'Guided lesson';q=source?.question||'';model=source?.choices?.[source.answer]||'';why=source?.reason||'';hint=source?.hint||'';extras=[source?.goal,source?.hook,source?.rule,source?.example].filter(Boolean).join(' · ').slice(0,600);
  }else if(v21TrailActive){s=v21TrailSubject||s;mode='original guided course';let guide=v21CurrentItem?.();title=guide?.title||title;q=guide?.content?.question||'';why=guide?.content?.why||'';extras=(guide?.content?.plain||'').slice(0,550);
  }else if(activePath){mode='archived course';title=v28El('pageTitle')?.textContent||activePath;extras=activePath;const frame=v28El('screen')?.querySelector('iframe');if(frame){const doc=frame.contentDocument;const main=doc?.querySelector('main,article,[role="main"],body');if(main)extras+=' · '+(main.innerText||'').slice(0,700)} }
 }catch(e){console.warn('Tutor context limited',e)}
 const chosen=(window.getSelection?.()?.toString()||'').trim().slice(0,380);
 return {subject:s,mode,title,question:q,modelAnswer:String(model),explanation:String(why),clue:String(hint),background:String(extras).slice(0,800),round,selectedText:chosen};
}
function v28CtxKey(c){return c.subject+'|'+c.mode+'|'+c.title+'|'+c.round}
function v28SetStatus(){const c=v28Context(),key=v28CtxKey(c);if(v28LastContextKey&&key!==v28LastContextKey){v28Focus=null;v28StepCounter=0;}v28LastContextKey=key;const mode=v28Config.useOnline&&v28Config.endpoint?'Online AI when connected · offline fallback':'Offline lesson coach';v28El('v28Context').textContent=`${c.subject.toUpperCase()} · ${c.title}${c.round?' · round '+c.round:''} — ${mode}`;return c}
function v28Message(role,text){const entry={role,text:String(text||'').slice(0,3500)};v28Messages.push(entry);v28Messages=v28Messages.slice(-45);try{safeStore.set('v28:tutor-history',v28Messages.slice(-35))}catch(_){}v28DrawMessage(entry);return entry}
function v28DrawMessage(m){const log=v28El('v28Log');if(!log)return;const div=document.createElement('div');div.className='v28-message '+(m.role==='user'?'v28-user':'v28-assistant');div.innerHTML=`<span class="v28-role">${m.role==='user'?'You':'Your teacher'}</span>${v28Escape(m.text)}`;log.append(div);log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight}
function v28Welcome(){return 'Ask me about the lesson in front of you. I can explain the idea from zero, work through the answer, give a hint, or check your thinking.\n\nAsk about the lessons here, or connect a local tutor in settings for more open-ended questions.'}
function v28Intent(message){const n=v28Normalized(message);if(/\b(answer|solution|correct response|correct move|tell me the answer|show me the answer)\b/.test(n))return 'answer';if(/\b(step by step|walk me|walk through|one step|next step|slowly|break it down|break into|how do i solve)\b/.test(n))return 'steps';if(/\b(quiz|test me|ask me|practice question|another question|give me a question)\b/.test(n))return 'quiz';if(/\b(hint|clue|nudge|dont tell me)\b/.test(n))return 'hint';if(/\b(mistake|wrong|incorrect|why did i get|what did i do wrong|my answer|i typed|i wrote|i said)\b/.test(n))return 'mistake';if(/\b(example|another example|show me how|use it in a sentence)\b/.test(n))return 'example';if(/\b(why|reason|point of|why does|why is)\b/.test(n))return 'why';if(/\b(simpl|confus|dont get|do not get|beginner|start from zero|never learned|basic|easier|lost|what is|what does|what are|means?|meaning)\b/.test(n))return 'simple';return 'explain'}
function v28TopicFor(message,ctx){const n=v28Normalized(message),pool=V28_TOPICS[ctx.subject]||[];let best=null,bestScore=0;
 for(const topic of pool){let score=0;for(const key of topic.keys){const phrase=v28Normalized(key);if(!phrase)continue;const at=n.indexOf(phrase);if(at>=0&&((at===0||n[at-1]===' ')&&(at+phrase.length===n.length||n[at+phrase.length]===' ')))score=Math.max(score,5+phrase.length/4);else if(phrase.length>=6&&n.includes(phrase))score=Math.max(score,4+phrase.length/4)}if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=topic}}
 if(best){if(v28Focus?.topic!==best)v28StepCounter=0;return best;}
 const follow=/^(why|how|what about|more|again|still|can you|i dont|i do not|one more|please|yes|no|okay|ok|huh|explain that|next)/.test(n)||['more','again','huh','yes','no','ok','okay'].includes(n);
 if(follow&&v28Focus?.subject===ctx.subject&&v28Focus.topic)return v28Focus.topic;
 const contextText=v28Normalized([ctx.title,ctx.question,ctx.background].join(' '));let guess=null,score=0;for(const t of pool)for(const key of t.keys){const k=v28Normalized(key);if(k.length>=4&&contextText.includes(k)&&k.length>score){score=k.length;guess=t}}
 return guess;
}
function v28TopicReply(topic,intent,ctx){v28Focus={subject:ctx.subject,topic};if(intent==='quiz'){return `Try this before checking the answer:\n\n${ctx.question||'Explain '+topic.title+' in your own words and give one example.'}\n\nReply with what you think, and I can compare it with the model. You can also select “Show answer” at any time.`}
 if(intent==='steps'){const pos=Math.min(v28StepCounter,topic.steps.length-1),content=topic.steps[pos];v28StepCounter++;return `${topic.title} — step ${pos+1} of ${topic.steps.length}\n\n${content}\n\n${pos+1<topic.steps.length?'Ask “next step” when that part makes sense.':`Example: ${topic.example}`}`}
 if(intent==='hint')return `Try this first: ${topic.steps[0]}\n\nThen have another go at the exercise. I won't spoil the answer unless you ask.`;
 if(intent==='example')return `${topic.title}\n\n${topic.example}\n\nThe general idea: ${topic.simple}`;
 if(intent==='mistake')return `${topic.title}\n\nCommon trap: ${topic.mistake}\n\nModel answer to the current exercise: ${ctx.modelAnswer||'not available in this lesson'}.\n\nTell me what you wrote or tried if you want to compare your reasoning.`;
 if(intent==='why')return `${topic.title}\n\n${topic.simple}\n\nFor example: ${topic.example}${ctx.explanation?'\n\nIn this lesson: '+ctx.explanation:''}`;
 return `${topic.title}\n\n${topic.simple}\n\n${topic.example}\n\n${topic.mistake?'Something to watch for: '+topic.mistake:''}\n\nAsk “one step at a time” if you want to build it slowly.`}
function v28KnownAnswer(ctx){if(ctx.modelAnswer){return `Here is the model answer to the question in front of you:\n\n${ctx.question?'Question: '+ctx.question+'\n\n':''}${ctx.modelAnswer}\n\nWhy it works: ${ctx.explanation||ctx.clue||'This is the stored model from the course. Ask about the specific step that confuses you.'}\n\nLooking at an answer is studying; it won't be counted as independent mastery.`}return 'I cannot find a verified model answer for the current archived activity. If you open a guided exercise, I can show its authored solution. I won’t make up an answer to an unknown question.'}
function v28LessonReply(intent,ctx){const model=ctx.modelAnswer,why=ctx.explanation,clue=ctx.clue||'Start by identifying the rule you need, then check one step at a time.';
 if(intent==='answer')return v28KnownAnswer(ctx);
 if(intent==='quiz')return ctx.question?`Let’s check what you understand. Try the current question without revealing the answer:\n\n${ctx.question}\n\nSend me what you tried, or ask for a hint.`:null;
 if(intent==='hint')return `Clue for “${ctx.title}”: ${clue}\n\nTry again, or ask me to reveal the worked answer.`;
 if(intent==='steps'){const steps=[ctx.background&&ctx.background.length<200?ctx.background:clue,clue,why,model?'The model answer is: '+model:'Check the lesson for the worked solution'].filter((s,i,a)=>s&&a.indexOf(s)===i);if(!steps.length)return null;const pos=Math.min(v28StepCounter,steps.length-1);v28StepCounter++;return `Step ${pos+1} of ${steps.length}: ${steps[pos]}\n\n${pos+1<steps.length?'Ask “next step” for the next part.':'You can practice this again without looking.'}`}
 if(intent==='why')return why?`Why this works in “${ctx.title}”:\n\n${why}${model?'\n\nThe model answer: '+model:''}`:null;
 if(intent==='example')return ctx.question&&model?`Here's a worked example from your lesson:\n\n${ctx.question}\n\n${model}\n\n${why||clue}`:null;
 if(intent==='mistake'){const attempted=v28El('v27Typed')?.value||v28El('v27VerbInput')?.value||'';return model?`Let's compare your attempt with the example.\n\n${attempted?'You entered: '+attempted+'\n':''}Model: ${model}\n\n${clue}\n\n${why||'Tell me which part differs, and I’ll explain the governing rule.'}\n\nNote: Offline checking can miss valid alternative answers, especially in Spanish.`:null}
 if(intent==='simple')return `Let's make “${ctx.title}” manageable.\n\n${ctx.background.length<220&&ctx.background?ctx.background+'\n\n':''}${clue}\n\n${why?'The key idea: '+why:''}\n\nYou can ask for the example or reveal the complete answer.`;
 return ctx.question?`You're working on “${ctx.title}.”\n\n${ctx.question}\n\nStart here: ${clue}\n\n${why||''}\n\nTell me which word, step, or choice is confusing.`:null;
}

function v28ArchiveHelp(message,ctx){
  try{
    if(typeof resultsFor!=='function')return null;
    const docs=resultsFor(message,ctx.subject);
    if(!docs?.length)return null;
    const match=docs[0];const source=String(match.body||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    const key=v28Normalized(message).split(' ').filter(w=>w.length>4&&!V28_STOP.has(w))[0];
    let at=key?v28Normalized(source).indexOf(key):-1;if(at<0)at=0;
    const excerpt=source.slice(Math.max(0,at-90),Math.max(0,at-90)+750).trim();
    if(!excerpt)return null;
    return `I found a relevant passage in the preserved ${ctx.subject} materials, though this is an excerpt rather than an AI-generated explanation.\n\n${match.title}:\n\n“${excerpt}${source.length>excerpt.length?'…':''}”\n\nIf you need a fresh explanation or a question about something the original course doesn't cover, use Online AI in Settings.`;
  }catch(_){return null}
}

function v28OfflineAnswer(message,ctx){const intent=v28Intent(message),normalized=v28Normalized(message),topic=v28TopicFor(message,ctx);
 if(intent==='answer')return v28KnownAnswer(ctx);
 if(/\bnext step\b/.test(normalized)&&v28Focus?.topic)return v28TopicReply(v28Focus.topic,'steps',ctx);
 if(topic&&v28TopicFor(message,{...ctx,title:'',question:'',background:''})===topic)return v28TopicReply(topic,intent,ctx);
 if(normalized.includes('i wrote')||normalized.includes('i typed')||normalized.includes('my answer'))return v28LessonReply('mistake',ctx)||'Tell me the complete question and what you tried, and I’ll compare it with a verified answer.';
 if(topic&&(['why','simple','example','steps','mistake'].includes(intent))&&(!ctx.question||ctx.mode==='overview'))return v28TopicReply(topic,intent,ctx);
 const referenceTerms=['this','that','it','here','current','lesson','answer','question','exercise','example','my','i','me','problem','step','clue','the','why'];const refersToCurrent=referenceTerms.some(w=>normalized.split(' ').includes(w))||normalized.split(' ').some(w=>w.length>=5&&v28Normalized(ctx.title+' '+ctx.question).includes(w));const questionTerms=normalized.split(' ').filter(w=>w.length>=5&&!V28_STOP.has(w));const specificQuestion=questionTerms.length>=2&&!refersToCurrent;const lesson=!specificQuestion||['answer','hint'].includes(intent)?v28LessonReply(intent,ctx):null;if(lesson)return lesson;
 if(topic)return v28TopicReply(topic,intent,ctx);
 const keywords=normalized.split(' ').filter(w=>w.length>4&&!V28_STOP.has(w));let relevant=[];try{if(typeof COACH_BANK!=='undefined')relevant=(COACH_BANK[ctx.subject]||[]).filter(c=>keywords.some(w=>v28Normalized(c.key+' '+c.aliases.join(' ')).includes(w))).slice(0,2)}catch(_){};
 if(relevant.length){const c=relevant[0];return `${c.key}\n\n${c.foundation||c.plain||''}\n\n${c.example?'Example: '+c.example:''}\n\nYou can ask for a simpler explanation or a worked example.`}
 const grounded=v28ArchiveHelp(message,ctx);if(grounded)return grounded;
 return `I don't have a reliable offline explanation for that specific question${ctx.mode!=='overview'?' in this lesson':''}.\n\nI can still explain the current exercise, show its answer, or walk through the steps. For arbitrary follow-up questions, enable Online AI in Settings using a secure tutor endpoint (not an API key).`;
}
async function v28Send(question){const q=String(question||'').trim();if(!q||v28Busy)return;v28El('v28Input').value='';const ctx=v28SetStatus();v28Message('user',q);v28Busy=true;v28El('v28Send').disabled=true;
 try{if(v28Config.useOnline&&v28Config.endpoint){
  const ctrl=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(),28000);
  try{const history=v28Messages.slice(-12).map(({role,text})=>({role,content:text}));const response=await fetch(v28Config.endpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({messages:history,context:ctx}),signal:ctrl.signal});if(!response.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+response.status);const data=await response.json();if(!data||typeof data.reply!=='string'||!data.reply.trim())throw Error('Empty tutor reply');v28Message('assistant',data.reply.slice(0,4500))}
  catch(e){v28Message('assistant','Online AI is not reachable ('+String(e.message||'network error')+'). Here is the built-in lesson guidance instead:\n\n'+v28OfflineAnswer(q,ctx))}
  finally{clearTimeout(timer)}
 }else v28Message('assistant',v28OfflineAnswer(q,ctx))}
 finally{v28Busy=false;v28El('v28Send').disabled=false;v28El('v28Input').focus()}
}
function v28Open(text=''){if(!v28El('v28Panel'))return;v28El('v28Panel').hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('v28-chat-open');if(v28El('coach'))v28El('coach').hidden=true;if(v28El('v25UniversalAnswer'))v28El('v25UniversalAnswer').hidden=true;v28SetStatus();v28El('v28Input').focus();if(text){v28El('v28Input').value=text;v28El('v28Input').focus()}}
function v28Close(){v28El('v28Panel').hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('v28-chat-open');v28El('v28Launch').focus()}
function v28ContextRefresh(){if(v28El('v28Panel')&&!v28El('v28Panel').hidden)v28SetStatus()}
function v28AddInline(){const spots=document.querySelectorAll('.v27-help,.v23-help,.v24-tools,.v21-coach-actions');for(const el of spots){if(el.querySelector('.v28-inline-help'))continue;const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='v28-inline-help';b.textContent='Ask the tutor';b.onclick=()=>v28Open('');el.append(b)}}
function v28Init(){document.body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<button id="v28Launch" type="button" aria-haspopup="dialog" aria-controls="v28Panel">✦ Ask tutor</button><aside id="v28Panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="false" aria-label="Your teacher conversation" hidden><header class="v28-head"><div><strong>Your teacher</strong><small>Ask about a lesson or something you got stuck on.</small></div><div class="v28-head-actions"><button type="button" id="v28SettingsToggle" aria-label="Tutor settings" title="Tutor settings">⚙</button><button type="button" id="v28Close" aria-label="Close tutor">✕</button></div></header><div id="v28Context" aria-live="polite"></div><div id="v28Log" role="log" aria-label="Tutor messages" aria-live="polite"></div><div class="v28-quick"><button data-v28-question="Explain this like I'm new to it">Explain simply</button><button data-v28-question="Walk me through it one step at a time">One step at a time</button><button data-v28-question="Give me a hint, not the answer">A hint</button><button data-v28-question="Show me the correct answer and explain why">Show answer</button><button data-v28-question="Why does this work?">Why?</button></div><div class="v28-compose"><textarea rows="2" id="v28Input" placeholder="What’s confusing? Ask in your own words…" aria-label="Your question"></textarea><button type="button" id="v28Send">Ask</button></div><div class="v28-foot"><span id="v28FootStatus">Offline help built from your lessons</span><button type="button" id="v28Clear">Clear chat</button></div><div id="v28Settings" hidden><h3>Online AI (optional)</h3><p>For longer, open-ended conversations, you can connect a tutor in settings. Do not paste an API key into this app.</p><label for="v28Endpoint">Secure tutor endpoint</label><input type="url" id="v28Endpoint" placeholder="https://your-domain.com/api/tutor" autocomplete="off"><label><input type="checkbox" id="v28UseOnline"> Use online AI for questions (sends the question and lesson context to your server)</label><button id="v28SaveConfig" type="button">Save connection</button><p id="v28ConfigMessage" role="status"></p></div></aside>`);
 try{v28Config={...v28Config,...(safeStore.get('v28:config',{})||{})};if(!v28Config.endpoint&&location.protocol==='https:')v28Config.endpoint=location.origin+'/api/tutor';v28Messages=(safeStore.get('v28:tutor-history',[])||[]).filter(m=>['assistant','user'].includes(m.role)&&typeof m.text==='string').slice(-35)}catch(_){};
 if(!v28Messages.length)v28Messages=[{role:'assistant',text:v28Welcome()}];v28Messages.forEach(v28DrawMessage);
 v28El('v28Launch').onclick=()=>v28Open();v28El('v28Close').onclick=v28Close;
 v28El('v28Send').onclick=()=>v28Send(v28El('v28Input').value);
 v28El('v28Input').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();v28Send(e.currentTarget.value)}};
 document.querySelectorAll('[data-v28-question]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v28Send(b.dataset.v28Question));
 v28El('v28Clear').onclick=()=>{v28Messages=[];v28Focus=null;v28StepCounter=0;v28El('v28Log').innerHTML='';v28Message('assistant',v28Welcome())};
 v28El('v28SettingsToggle').onclick=()=>{const pane=v28El('v28Settings');pane.hidden=!pane.hidden;if(!pane.hidden){v28El('v28Endpoint').value=v28Config.endpoint||'';v28El('v28UseOnline').checked=!!v28Config.useOnline}};
 v28El('v28SaveConfig').onclick=()=>{const raw=v28El('v28Endpoint').value.trim(),check=v28El('v28UseOnline').checked;let valid=true;try{if(raw){const u=new URL(raw);valid=u.protocol==='https:'||u.protocol==='http:'&&['localhost','127.0.0.1'].includes(u.hostname)}}catch(_){valid=false}if(!valid){v28El('v28ConfigMessage').textContent='Use an HTTPS URL (or localhost for testing). Never put an API key in the address.';return}if(check&&!raw){v28El('v28ConfigMessage').textContent='Add a secure server endpoint before enabling online AI.';return}v28Config={endpoint:raw,useOnline:check};safeStore.set('v28:config',v28Config);v28El('v28ConfigMessage').textContent=check?'Connection saved. Online AI will be used when the endpoint is reachable.':'Saved. The tutor will use offline lesson help.';v28SetStatus()};
 document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&!v28El('v28Panel').hidden&&v28El('v28Settings').hidden)v28Close()});
 const fns=['v27StartStep','v27VerbRenderQuestion','v23Render','showHome','go','v27Open','v27VerbOpen'];for(const key of fns){try{const fn=window[key];if(typeof fn!=='function')continue;window[key]=function(...args){const ret=fn.apply(this,args);v28ContextRefresh();v28AddInline();return ret}}catch(e){console.warn('Cannot wrap '+key,e)}}
 v28AddInline();
}
v28Init();


/* V29: responsive in-lesson tutor. The examples are drawn from the lessons. */
const V29_STORE='v29:learning-notes';
let v29Quiz=null,v29TurnContext='',v29HelpStage=0,v29QuestionCount=0,v29LastWrong=null;
function v29Current(){let c=v28Context();return c}
function v29ID(c){return [c.subject,c.mode,c.title,c.round,c.question].join('|')}
function v29Safe(s){return v28Escape(String(s??''))}
function v29Note(kind,detail=''){
 const c=v29Current(),key=v29ID(c),all=safeStore.get(V29_STORE,{})||{};
 const item=all[key]||{subject:c.subject,title:c.title,question:c.question,created:Date.now(),seen:0,helped:0,correct:0};
 item.seen++;item.last=Date.now();item[kind]=(item[kind]||0)+1;item.detail=String(detail).slice(0,200);all[key]=item;
 const entries=Object.entries(all).sort((a,b)=>b[1].last-a[1].last).slice(0,200);
 safeStore.set(V29_STORE,Object.fromEntries(entries));
}
function v29MarkHelp(reason){
 const c=v29Current();
 if(v27Active&&v27Round&&!v27Round.done){v27Round.assisted=true;const feedback=document.getElementById('v27Feedback');if(feedback&&!feedback.textContent.includes('tutor'))feedback.dataset.tutorAssisted='true'}
 if(c.mode==='verb studio')v27VerbAssisted=true;
 v29Note('helped',reason)
}
function v29Sync(){
 const c=v29Current(),id=v29ID(c);
 if(id!==v29TurnContext){v29Quiz=null;v29LastWrong=null;v29HelpStage=0;v29TurnContext=id;v29RefreshActions()}
 const mode=(v28Config.useOnline&&v28Config.endpoint)?'Online AI connected when available':'Offline: course-grounded coaching';
 const holder=document.getElementById('v28Context');let badge=document.getElementById('v29LessonBadge');if(holder&&!badge){badge=document.createElement('div');badge.id='v29LessonBadge';holder.append(badge)}if(badge)badge.textContent=c.question?`${c.title} · Question ${c.round||1} · ${mode}`:`${c.title} · ${mode}`;
 return c;
}
function v29TeachSteps(c){
 const type=v27Active&&v27StepInfo()?.d?.type||'';
 const s=c.subject;
 if(s==='spanish'||c.mode==='verb studio')return [
  ['Find the job of the sentence','What are you trying to say—something happening now, something completed, a habit, a hope, or a request?'],
  ['Choose the pattern',c.clue||'Identify who is doing the action, then decide which tense or mood fits.'],
  ['Build the sentence','Say the subject and verb aloud. Add any pronouns where Spanish naturally puts them.'],
  ['Check the model',`The example answer is “${c.modelAnswer||'not supplied'}”. ${c.explanation||'Notice how the chosen form matches the meaning.'}`]
 ];
 if(s==='piano')return [
  ['Get oriented','Find a pair of black keys. The white key just before the pair is C. C4 is middle C.'],
  ['Practice one small piece',c.clue||'Locate the first note, then move to the next without rushing.'],
  ['Keep a steady pulse','Play slowly enough to name every note. Speed comes later.'],
  ['Check the pattern',`The stored notes are ${c.modelAnswer||'not available'}. ${c.explanation||''}`]
 ];
 if(s==='chess')return [
  ['Look for danger','Which side is to move? Are there checks or immediate threats?'],
  ['Look for forcing moves',c.clue||'Consider checks, captures, attacks and safe pieces before guessing.'],
  ['Check the reply','Ask what your opponent can do immediately after the candidate move.'],
  ['Compare with the source',`The lesson move is ${c.modelAnswer||'not available'}. ${c.explanation||''}`]
 ];
 if(s==='calculus'||s==='physics')return [
  ['Translate the question','List what is known, what is unknown, and any units.'],
  ['Choose one idea',c.clue||'Find the relationship that connects what you know to what you need.'],
  ['Work one operation','Put numbers and units into the relationship, then compute carefully.'],
  ['Check the result',`The stored result is ${c.modelAnswer||'not available'}. ${c.explanation||''}`]
 ];
 return [
  ['Find the question','What exactly is being asked? Restate it in ordinary language.'],
  ['Find the key idea',c.clue||'Locate one relevant principle from the course.'],
  ['Apply it','Explain your reasoning before checking the finished answer.'],
  ['Check and compare',`The lesson model is ${c.modelAnswer||'not provided'}. ${c.explanation||''}`]
 ];
}
function v29ArchivedAnswer(c){
 if(c.mode!=='archived course')return null;
 try{
  const iframe=document.getElementById('frame')||document.querySelector('#screen iframe');
  const doc=iframe?.contentDocument; if(!doc)return null;
  const toggle=doc.getElementById('v25ArchiveToggle');
  if(!toggle)return null;
  if(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')!=='true')toggle.click();
  const content=doc.getElementById('v25ArchiveContent');
  const txt=content?.innerText?.trim();
  if(!txt)return null;
  if(txt.includes('No verified answer is provided'))return 'There is no verified answer for this particular archive screen. It may be a reading or an open-ended question. I will not invent one.';
  return `Here is the answer supplied by the original course:\n\n${txt}\n\nThe same worked answer is open in the course itself.`;
 }catch(e){return null}
}
function v29Teach(c,kind){
 const steps=v29TeachSteps(c);
 if(kind==='small')return `Here is the simple version. ${c.clue||c.explanation||'Start with what the question is asking, not the technical term.'}\n\nWe can work through this together. Choose “Walk me through” for one small step at a time.`;
 if(kind==='one'){v29MarkHelp('walkthrough');const i=Math.min(v29HelpStage,steps.length-1);v29HelpStage++;const [title,body]=steps[i];return `${title} (${i+1}/${steps.length})\n\n${body}\n\n${i===steps.length-1?'Now try a different question without looking.':'Try that part. Choose “Next step” when you’re ready.'}`}
 if(kind==='hint'){v29MarkHelp('hint');return `One clue, without the answer:\n\n${c.clue||steps[1][1]}\n\nTry it in the lesson. If it still isn't clicking, choose “Walk me through”.`}
 if(kind==='answer'){v29MarkHelp('answer revealed');const archive=v29ArchivedAnswer(c);if(archive)return archive;return v28KnownAnswer(c)+`\n\nNext: choose “Test me” to try without copying, or return to the lesson.`}
 if(kind==='why'){v29MarkHelp('explained model');return c.explanation?`The central reason:\n\n${c.explanation}\n\n${c.clue?'A useful clue: '+c.clue+'\n\n':''}Want to prove it? Choose “Test me”.`:`The lesson has no separately verified reasoning here. ${c.clue||''}\n\nI can show the authored answer, but I won't invent a proof.`}
 return v28OfflineAnswer('Explain this simply',c)
}
function v29Model(c){
 if(!c.modelAnswer)return null;
 let d=null;
 try{if(v27Active)d=v27StepInfo()}catch(_){}
 if(d){const typ=d.d.type;
  if(typ==='choice'||(typ==='chess-board'&&d.second))return {type:'choice',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,options:d.options||[],correct:d.correct,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
  if(typ==='number')return {type:'number',prompt:c.question,model:String(c.modelAnswer),tolerance:Number(d.d.tolerance)||.00001,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
  if(typ==='piano')return {type:'piano',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
  if(typ==='chess-board')return {type:'chess',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
  const variants=d.second?d.d.secondVariants:d.d.variants;return {type:'typed',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,why:c.explanation,variants:variants||[]}
 }
 if(c.mode==='verb studio')return {type:'typed',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
 if(c.mode==='intro guided lesson')return {type:'choice',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,options:V23_TRACKS?.[c.subject]?.[v23Active?.index]?.choices||[],correct:V23_TRACKS?.[c.subject]?.[v23Active?.index]?.answer,why:c.explanation,variants:[]};
 return {type:'typed',prompt:c.question,model:c.modelAnswer,why:c.explanation,variants:[]}
}
function v29Check(q,a){
 const val=String(a||'').trim();if(!val)return {state:'empty',response:'Type an answer or choose an option.'};
 if(q.type==='number'){
  const n=Number(val.replace(/,/g,'')),wanted=Number(q.model);
  if(!Number.isFinite(n))return {state:'wrong',response:'That needs to be a number. Identify which quantity the problem asks for and check the units.'};
  if(Math.abs(n-wanted)<=q.tolerance)return {state:'correct',response:`Exactly: ${q.model}. ${q.why}`};
  return {state:'wrong',response:`Not quite. You entered ${val}. Check the setup and units first. ${q.why?'Useful idea: '+q.why:''} You can reveal the worked model whenever you want.`}
 }
 if(q.type==='choice'){
   let index=q.options.findIndex(op=>v28Normalized(op)===v28Normalized(val));
   if(/^\d+$/.test(val)&&Number(val)>=1&&Number(val)<=q.options.length)index=Number(val)-1;
   if(index<0)return {state:'uncertain',response:'I can check one of the listed choices. Pick an option above so we can compare it reliably.'};
   if(index===Number(q.correct))return {state:'correct',response:`Yes — ${q.options[index]}. ${q.why||''}`};
   return {state:'wrong',response:`You chose “${q.options[index]}”. Think about the distinction in the question before revealing the answer. ${q.why?'Relevant rule: '+q.why:''}`};
 }
 let normalized=s=>v28Normalized(s).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
 const forms=[q.model,...q.variants];
 if(forms.some(s=>normalized(s)===normalized(val)))return {state:'correct',response:`Yes — that matches the course model. ${q.why||''}`};
 if(q.type==='chess'){const move=normalized(val).replace(/[^a-h1-8]/g,'');if(move.length===4){return {state:'wrong',response:`You selected ${move.slice(0,2)} → ${move.slice(2)}. The stored tactic uses a different move. Check that you started from one of your own pieces, then look at forcing checks and captures. ${q.why?'Underlying idea: '+q.why:''}`}}}
 if(q.type==='piano'||q.type==='chess'){
   const simplified=s=>normalized(s).replace(/\bto\b|→|\-|\>/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,'');
   if(forms.some(s=>simplified(s)===simplified(val)))return {state:'correct',response:`Correct pattern. ${q.why||''}`};
 }
 if(q.type==='typed'){
   const tokens=normalized(val).split(' '),answerTokens=normalized(q.model).split(' ');
   if(tokens.length===answerTokens.length){const errors=tokens.map((t,i)=>({t,want:answerTokens[i],i})).filter(x=>x.t!==x.want);
     if(errors.length===1){let e=errors[0];return {state:'wrong',response:`You're very close. Focus on word ${e.i+1}: “${e.t}”. Check the form or ending and try again. ${q.why?'Underlying rule: '+q.why:''}`};}
   }
   const overlap=tokens.filter(t=>answerTokens.includes(t)).length;
   if(overlap>=Math.max(1,Math.floor(answerTokens.length/2)))return {state:'wrong',response:`Much of that matches the model, but there's a meaningful difference. Check the verb form, word order, or whether you chose the right tense. ${q.why?'Underlying rule: '+q.why:''}`};
 }
 return {state:'uncertain',response:'That’s different from the example answer. Another answer might also work—There may be other correct ways to say it. Try the course model or use the connected AI tutor for flexible checking.'};
}
function v29Card(){return document.getElementById('v29PracticeCard')}
function v29QuizStart(){const c=v29Sync(),q=v29Model(c);if(!q){v28Message('assistant','I can’t check an answer from this page. Open a practice lesson if you’d like a question I can check.');return}
 v29Quiz={...q,context:v29ID(c)};v29QuestionCount++;v29Note('seen','tutor practice');
 v28Message('assistant','Try this without looking at the model. I’ll compare it with the stored lesson answer. This chat practice does not change your course mastery score.');
 let card=v29Card();if(!card){card=document.createElement('section');card.id='v29PracticeCard';card.className='v29-practice';v28El('v28Log').append(card)}
 card.innerHTML=`<span class="v29-card-kicker">PRACTICE WITH YOUR TUTOR</span><p>${v29Safe(q.prompt||c.title)}</p>${q.type==='choice'?`<div class="v29-card-choices">${q.options.map((v,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v29-option="${i}">${v29Safe(v)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`:`<div class="v29-card-entry"><input id="v29Attempt" type="text" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Your attempt" placeholder="Try your answer"><button id="v29Check">Check</button></div>`}<div id="v29PracticeFeedback" role="status"></div><div class="v29-card-bottom"><button type="button" id="v29PracticeHint">A clue</button><button type="button" id="v29PracticeReveal">Show answer + why</button><button type="button" id="v29Return">Back to lesson ↗</button></div>`;
 if(q.type==='choice')card.querySelectorAll('[data-v29-option]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v29QuizCheck(q.options[Number(b.dataset.v29Option)]));
 else{card.querySelector('#v29Check').onclick=()=>v29QuizCheck(card.querySelector('#v29Attempt').value);card.querySelector('#v29Attempt').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')v29QuizCheck(e.currentTarget.value)}}
 card.querySelector('#v29PracticeHint').onclick=()=>{v29MarkHelp('chat practice clue');card.querySelector('#v29PracticeFeedback').textContent=c.clue||v29TeachSteps(c)[1][1]};
 card.querySelector('#v29PracticeReveal').onclick=()=>{v29MarkHelp('chat practice reveal');card.querySelector('#v29PracticeFeedback').textContent='Model answer: '+q.model+'\n\nWhy: '+(q.why||c.clue||'This is the stored answer from the course.')};
 card.querySelector('#v29Return').onclick=()=>v29BackToLesson();
 v28El('v28Log').scrollTop=v28El('v28Log').scrollHeight;
}
function v29QuizCheck(value){
 if(!v29Quiz)return;
 const c=v29Sync();if(v29ID(c)!==v29Quiz?.context){v29Quiz=null;const card=v29Card();if(card)card.remove();v28Message('assistant','You changed lessons. I cleared that old practice question so I don’t grade it against the wrong answer.');return}
 const outcome=v29Check(v29Quiz,value),fb=v29Card()?.querySelector('#v29PracticeFeedback');if(fb){fb.textContent=outcome.response;fb.className='v29-result-'+outcome.state}
 if(outcome.state==='correct')v29Note('correct','chat check');else if(outcome.state==='wrong')v29Note('missed','chat check');
 if(outcome.state==='correct'&&fb)fb.textContent+='\n\nNow try it in the main lesson for tracked progress.';
}
function v29BackToLesson(){v28Close();const el=document.querySelector('#v27Typed,#v27VerbInput,#v27Activity .v27-piano-tools,#v27Activity .v27-chess-board,#v27Activity .v27-options,#v27Work');if(el){el.scrollIntoView({block:'center',behavior:'smooth'});if(el.matches('input'))el.focus()}}
function v29Diagnose(mode){const c=v29Sync();v29MarkHelp('diagnosis '+mode);
 let response='';const steps=v29TeachSteps(c);
 if(mode==='words')response=`Let's decode what the question is asking.\n\n${c.question||c.title}\n\n${c.subject==='spanish'?'First find the verb, who performs it, and whether it is a statement, request, or hope.':c.subject==='piano'?'First find the named keys. A group of two black keys marks a C immediately to its left.':c.subject==='chess'?'First identify whose move it is, then look for checks and threats.':c.subject==='calculus'||c.subject==='physics'?'First find the quantities given and the exact value you need.':steps[0][1]}\n\nWhich specific word or part is unclear?`;
 if(mode==='rule')response=`Here's the principle to look for:\n\n${c.clue||steps[1][1]}\n\nI can explain that in simpler terms or walk through it one step at a time.`;
 if(mode==='apply')response=`You know the idea—now connect it to this question.\n\n1. ${steps[0][1]}\n2. ${steps[1][1]}\n\nTry just the first step. You do not have to solve everything in one go.`;
 if(mode==='mistake'){const attempt=v29LastWrong?.id===v29ID(c)?v29LastWrong:null;if(attempt){const q=v29Model(c),result=q?v29Check(q,attempt.value):null;response=`I can see your last attempt: “${attempt.value}”\n\n${result?.response||'Let’s go back through the method carefully.'}\n\nThis comparison uses the prepared answer, not an open-ended language evaluator.`}else response=`Let's inspect what happened, not just say “wrong.”\n\n${c.question||'Current question'}\n\nTell me exactly what you entered or chose. I'll compare it with the verified model where possible and identify the likely point of difference.`;}
 v28Message('assistant',response)
}
function v29RefreshActions(){const card=v29Card();if(card)card.remove();}
function v29CompareAttempt(text){const c=v29Sync(),q=v29Model(c);if(!q)return null;const match=text.match(/(?:i (?:wrote|typed|answered|chose|said|tried)|my answer is|i think(?: it's| it is)?|check this|check my answer)\s*[:：\-]?\s*([\s\S]+)/i);if(!match||!match[1]?.trim())return null;
 const candidate=match[1].trim(),outcome=v29Check(q,candidate);if(outcome.state==='wrong')v29Note('missed','answer comparison');if(outcome.state==='correct')v29Note('correct','answer comparison');return `You tried: “${candidate}”\n\n${outcome.response}\n\nThis comparison is to the stored course model. For other valid interpretations, use connected AI or a teacher.`}
function v29ChatQuestion(q){const question=String(q||'').trim();if(!question||v28Busy)return;
 v29Sync();const c=v29Current(),n=v28Normalized(question);v28El('v28Input').value='';v28Message('user',question);
 const isQuick=['what part','where to start','not understand','i am stuck','i m stuck','im stuck','stuck','confused','lost','help'].some(s=>n.includes(s));
 const isHelp=/^(explain|how do|how does|walk|step|next step|why|show|hint|give|i don|i do not|what|can you|help|teach|simplify|test|quiz|ask)/.test(n);
 // Any contextual assistance in the current guided round makes that round assisted.
 if(isQuick||isHelp||/\b(answer|solution|model)\b/.test(n))v29MarkHelp('tutor assistance');
 if(/\b(why was i wrong|why am i wrong|where did i go wrong|what did i do wrong|explain my mistake|why didn't that work|help with my mistake)\b/.test(n)&&v29LastWrong?.id===v29ID(c)){v29MarkHelp('mistake review');const q=v29Model(c),result=q?v29Check(q,v29LastWrong.value):null;v28Message('assistant',`You tried: “${v29LastWrong.value}”\n\n${result?.response||'I cannot reliably check that against a verified model.'}\n\nWe can revisit the relevant rule or break it down further.`);return}
 const comparison=v29CompareAttempt(question);
 if(comparison){if(v28Config.useOnline&&v28Config.endpoint&&comparison.includes('legitimate alternative'))v29OnlineQuestion(question,c);else v28Message('assistant',comparison);return}
 if(/\b(test me|quiz me|practice with|ask me (a|another)|check my understanding)\b/.test(n)){v29QuizStart();return}
 if(/\b(next step)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'one'));return}
 if(/\b(show|give|tell|reveal)\b.*\b(answer|solution|model|correct move)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'answer'));return}
 if(/\b(hint|clue|nudge)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'hint'));return}
 if(/\b(one step|step by step|walk me|walk through|break it down|slowly)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'one'));return}
 if(/\b(why does this|why is this|why does it|why is it|why does that|why is that|why this work|why is the answer)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'why'));return}
 if(/\b(i'm stuck|im stuck|i m stuck|i am stuck|confused|i dont get|i do not get|lost)\b/.test(n)){v28Message('assistant',`What is the hardest part right now? Choose a starting point below, and we'll deal with only that piece.\n\nYou can also ask your question in your own words.`);v29ShowDiagnosis();return}
 // Connected AI remains available for open-ended questions, with the same context.
 if(v28Config.useOnline&&v28Config.endpoint){v29OnlineQuestion(question,c);return}
 // Reuse V28 lesson explanations for subject-specific questions, with transparent limitations.
 const reply=v28OfflineAnswer(question,c);v28Message('assistant',reply);
}
async function v29OnlineQuestion(question,c){
 if(v28Busy)return;v28Busy=true;v28El('v28Send').disabled=true;
 try{
  const ctrl=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>ctrl.abort(),28000);
  try{const history=v28Messages.slice(-12).map(({role,text})=>({role,content:text}));const res=await fetch(v28Config.endpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({messages:history,context:{...c,learnerAskedForHelp:true,helpStage:v29HelpStage,learnerLastAttempt:v29LastWrong?.id===v29ID(c)?v29LastWrong.value:'',independencePolicy:'Any answer/hint/solution seen in this round is assisted. Do not claim independent mastery.'}}),signal:ctrl.signal});if(!res.ok)throw Error('HTTP '+res.status);const data=await res.json();if(!data||typeof data.reply!=='string')throw Error('No tutor reply');v28Message('assistant',data.reply.slice(0,4500));}
  catch(e){v28Message('assistant','The online tutor could not connect ('+String(e.message||'network issue')+'). Here is the lesson-based guidance instead:\n\n'+v28OfflineAnswer(question,c))}
  finally{clearTimeout(timer)}
 }finally{v28Busy=false;v28El('v28Send').disabled=false;v28El('v28Input').focus()}
}
function v29ShowDiagnosis(){let el=document.getElementById('v29Diagnosis');if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='v29Diagnosis';el.className='v29-diagnosis';v28El('v28Log').append(el)}el.innerHTML=`<p>Where is it breaking down?</p><div><button data-v29-confusion="words">I don't understand the question</button><button data-v29-confusion="rule">I don't know the rule</button><button data-v29-confusion="apply">I can't apply the rule</button><button data-v29-confusion="mistake">I got it wrong</button></div>`;
 el.querySelectorAll('[data-v29-confusion]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const mode=b.dataset.v29Confusion;el.remove();v29Diagnose(mode)});v28El('v28Log').scrollTop=v28El('v28Log').scrollHeight;
}
function v29OpenNotebook(){let all=safeStore.get(V29_STORE,{})||{};const c=v29Current();const entries=Object.entries(all).filter(([_,r])=>r.subject===c.subject).sort((a,b)=>b[1].last-a[1].last).slice(0,12);
 let area=document.getElementById('v29Notebook');if(!area){area=document.createElement('section');area.id='v29Notebook';area.className='v29-notebook';v28El('v28Panel').append(area)}
 area.innerHTML=`<header><strong>Questions to come back to</strong><button id="v29NotebookClose" aria-label="Close notes">×</button></header><p>Saved privately in this browser. A question needing help is a good review target—not a failure.</p>${entries.length?entries.map(([key,r])=>`<button class="v29-note-row" data-v29-key="${v29Safe(key)}"><strong>${v29Safe(r.title)}</strong><small>${r.helped||0} help requests · ${r.correct||0} tutor checks</small></button>`).join(''):'<p>No questions saved in this subject yet.</p>'}<button type="button" id="v29NotebookExport">Export my questions</button>`;
 area.querySelector('#v29NotebookClose').onclick=()=>area.remove();area.querySelector('#v29NotebookExport').onclick=()=>{const lines=entries.map(([k,r])=>`${r.subject} · ${r.title}\n${r.question||''}\nHelp: ${r.helped||0}; Checks: ${r.correct||0}`).join('\n\n');if(navigator.clipboard?.writeText){navigator.clipboard.writeText(lines).then(()=>v28Message('assistant','Review notes copied.')).catch(()=>v28Message('assistant',lines))}else v28Message('assistant',lines);area.remove()};
 area.querySelectorAll('[data-v29-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const k=b.dataset.v29Key,r=all[k];if(!r)return;v28Message('assistant',`Review this question:\n\n${r.question||r.title}\n\nGo back to that lesson when you are ready to solve it independently.`);area.remove()})
}
function v29Init(){
 if(!v28El('v28Panel'))return;
 const panel=v28El('v28Panel');panel.classList.add('v29-tutor');
 const head=panel.querySelector('.v28-head strong');if(head)head.textContent='Your learning coach';
 const sub=panel.querySelector('.v28-head small');if(sub)sub.textContent='Ask freely. Get unstuck. Then try it yourself.';
 const ctx=document.getElementById('v28Context');if(ctx){const badge=document.createElement('div');badge.id='v29LessonBadge';ctx.append(badge)}
 if(v28Messages.length===1&&v28Messages[0].role==='assistant'&&v28Messages[0].text.includes('This file has a built-in offline coach')){v28Messages[0].text='Tell me what isn’t clicking. I can show you one small step, explain the why, or check what you tried. You can always see the answer.\n\nWant to begin with “I’m stuck”?';const bubble=v28El('v28Log')?.querySelector('.v28-assistant');if(bubble)bubble.innerHTML='<span class="v28-role">Your teacher</span>'+v28Escape(v28Messages[0].text)}
 const q=panel.querySelector('.v28-quick');if(q){q.innerHTML=`<button id="v29Confused">I’m stuck</button><button id="v29OneStep">Walk me through</button><button id="v29NextStep">Next step</button><button id="v29TestMe">Test me</button><button id="v29ShowAnswer">Show answer + why</button>`;
 q.querySelector('#v29Confused').onclick=()=>{v29Sync();v29MarkHelp('stuck');v28Message('assistant','Tell me where it breaks down. We will work on just that piece.');v29ShowDiagnosis()};
 q.querySelector('#v29OneStep').onclick=()=>{const c=v29Sync();v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'one'))};
 q.querySelector('#v29NextStep').onclick=()=>{const c=v29Sync();v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'one'))};
 q.querySelector('#v29TestMe').onclick=v29QuizStart;
 q.querySelector('#v29ShowAnswer').onclick=()=>{const c=v29Sync();v28Message('assistant',v29Teach(c,'answer'))};
 }
 const foot=panel.querySelector('.v28-foot');if(foot){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v29Review';b.textContent='My trouble spots';b.onclick=v29OpenNotebook;foot.insertBefore(b,foot.querySelector('#v28Clear'))}
 v28El('v28Send').onclick=()=>v29ChatQuestion(v28El('v28Input').value);
 v28El('v28Input').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();v29ChatQuestion(e.currentTarget.value)}};
 const oldOpen=v28Open;v28Open=function(...args){const r=oldOpen.apply(this,args);v29Sync();return r};
 const oldStatus=v28SetStatus;v28SetStatus=function(...args){const r=oldStatus.apply(this,args);v29Sync();return r};
 // Capture the actual wrong answer in the exercise. Coaching can now diagnose it without requiring retyping.
 const baseSubmit=v27Submit;
 v27Submit=function(correct,given){
   const current=v29Current(),key=v29ID(current);
   const result=baseSubmit.apply(this,arguments);
   if(!correct){
     v29LastWrong={id:key,value:String(given??''),at:Date.now()};
     const feedback=document.getElementById('v27Feedback');
     if(feedback&&!document.getElementById('v29MistakeHelp')){
       const button=document.createElement('button');button.id='v29MistakeHelp';button.type='button';button.className='v29-mistake-help';button.textContent='Help me see what I missed →';
       feedback.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',button);
       button.onclick=()=>{v28Open();v29Diagnose('mistake')};
     }
   }else document.getElementById('v29MistakeHelp')?.remove();
   return result;
 };
 const baseVerbCheck=v27VerbCheck;
 v27VerbCheck=function(...args){
   const c=v29Current(),typed=document.getElementById('v27VerbInput')?.value||'',expected=v27VerbAnswer(v27VerbSelection.verb,v27VerbSelection.tense,v27VerbSelection.person);
   const output=baseVerbCheck.apply(this,args);
   if(typed.trim()&&!v27Equal(typed,[expected])){
      v29LastWrong={id:v29ID(c),value:typed.trim(),at:Date.now()};
      const fb=document.getElementById('v27VerbFeedback');
      if(fb&&!document.getElementById('v29MistakeHelp')){
        const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='v29MistakeHelp';b.className='v29-mistake-help';b.textContent='Help me see what I missed →';fb.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',b);b.onclick=()=>{v28Open();v29Diagnose('mistake')};
      }
   }else document.getElementById('v29MistakeHelp')?.remove();
   return output;
 };
 // In guided lessons, hints and revealed answers requested in chat cannot earn clean completion.
 // v27Round assisted is set before any authoritative response, and persists for that round.
 const oldVerbRender=v27VerbRenderQuestion;v27VerbRenderQuestion=function(...args){const result=oldVerbRender.apply(this,args);v29Sync();return result};
 const oldRound=v27StartStep;v27StartStep=function(...args){const result=oldRound.apply(this,args);v29Sync();return result};
 const oldClear=v28El('v28Clear').onclick;v28El('v28Clear').onclick=()=>{oldClear?.();v29Quiz=null;v29HelpStage=0;document.getElementById('v29Diagnosis')?.remove();v29RefreshActions()};
 const status=v28El('v28FootStatus');if(status)status.textContent='Offline explanations from lesson material · Online AI optional';
 v29Sync();
}
v29Init();

/* V30: legal move engine adapted from the preserved Study Table chess studio. */
const V30_CHESS_CORE=(function(){
function parseFen(fen){const [layout,turn='w',castling='-',ep='-']=fen.split(' ');const board=Array(64).fill(null);let n=0;for(const ch of layout){if(ch==='/')continue;if(/\d/.test(ch))n+=+ch;else board[n++]=ch}if(n!==64)throw Error('Bad FEN '+fen);return {board,turn,castling,ep};}
const sq2i=s=>8*(8-Number(s[1]))+s.charCodeAt(0)-97,i2sq=i=>String.fromCharCode(97+i%8)+(8-Math.floor(i/8)),color=p=>p?(p===p.toUpperCase()?'w':'b'):null,other=c=>c==='w'?'b':'w',pieceFor=(p,c)=>c==='w'?p.toUpperCase():p;
function attacked(s,by,state){const b=state.board,r=Math.floor(s/8),c=s%8;for(let i=0;i<64;i++){const p=b[i];if(!p||color(p)!==by)continue;const y=Math.floor(i/8),x=i%8,dr=r-y,dc=c-x,t=p.toLowerCase();if(t==='p'){if(dr===(by==='w'?-1:1)&&Math.abs(dc)===1)return true;continue}if(t==='n'){if((Math.abs(dr)===2&&Math.abs(dc)===1)||(Math.abs(dr)===1&&Math.abs(dc)===2))return true;continue}if(t==='k'){if(Math.max(Math.abs(dr),Math.abs(dc))===1)return true;continue}const straight=dr===0||dc===0,diag=Math.abs(dr)===Math.abs(dc);if(!((t==='r'&&straight)||(t==='b'&&diag)||(t==='q'&&(straight||diag))))continue;const sr=Math.sign(dr),sc=Math.sign(dc);let yy=y+sr,xx=x+sc,blocked=false;while(yy!==r||xx!==c){if(b[yy*8+xx]){blocked=true;break}yy+=sr;xx+=sc}if(!blocked)return true}return false;}
function checked(state,c){const i=state.board.findIndex(p=>p===pieceFor('k',c));return i<0||attacked(i,other(c),state)}
function pseudo(state,from){const b=state.board,p=b[from];if(!p)return[];const c=color(p),t=p.toLowerCase(),r=Math.floor(from/8),f=from%8,res=[];function add(y,x,special){if(y<0||y>7||x<0||x>7)return false;const to=y*8+x,q=b[to];if(q&&color(q)===c)return false;if(q&&q.toLowerCase()==='k')return false;res.push({from,to,special});return !q}function slide(dirs){for(const [dy,dx] of dirs){let y=r+dy,x=f+dx;while(y>=0&&y<8&&x>=0&&x<8){if(!add(y,x))break;y+=dy;x+=dx}}}
if(t==='r'||t==='q')slide([[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]);if(t==='b'||t==='q')slide([[1,1],[-1,-1],[1,-1],[-1,1]]);if(t==='n')for(const [dy,dx] of [[2,1],[2,-1],[-2,1],[-2,-1],[1,2],[1,-2],[-1,2],[-1,-2]])add(r+dy,f+dx);if(t==='k'){for(const dy of [-1,0,1])for(const dx of [-1,0,1])if(dy||dx)add(r+dy,f+dx);if(!checked(state,c)){const row=c==='w'?7:0;if(r===row&&f===4){for(const side of ['K','Q']){const right=c==='w'?side:side.toLowerCase(),rookCol=side==='K'?7:0,emptyCols=side==='K'?[5,6]:[1,2,3],crossCols=side==='K'?[5,6]:[3,2],rook=b[row*8+rookCol];if(state.castling.includes(right)&&rook===pieceFor('r',c)&&emptyCols.every(z=>!b[row*8+z])&&crossCols.every(z=>!attacked(row*8+z,other(c),state)))res.push({from,to:row*8+(side==='K'?6:2),special:'castle'});}}}}
if(t==='p'){const d=c==='w'?-1:1,promo=r+d===(c==='w'?0:7);if(r+d>=0&&r+d<8&&!b[(r+d)*8+f]){res.push({from,to:(r+d)*8+f,special:promo?'promote':null});const start=c==='w'?6:1;if(r===start&&!b[(r+2*d)*8+f])res.push({from,to:(r+2*d)*8+f,special:'double'})}for(const dx of [-1,1]){const x=f+dx,y=r+d;if(x<0||x>7||y<0||y>7)continue;const to=y*8+x,q=b[to];if(q&&color(q)===other(c)&&q.toLowerCase()!=='k')res.push({from,to,special:promo?'promote':null});else if(i2sq(to)===state.ep)res.push({from,to,special:'ep'});}}
return res;}
function apply(state,m){const s={board:state.board.slice(),turn:other(state.turn),castling:state.castling,ep:'-'};const p=s.board[m.from],taken=s.board[m.to],c=color(p),fr=Math.floor(m.from/8),fc=m.from%8,tr=Math.floor(m.to/8),tc=m.to%8;s.board[m.from]=null;s.board[m.to]=p;if(m.special==='ep')s.board[(fr*8+tc)]=null;if(m.special==='castle'){const row=fr;if(tc===6){s.board[row*8+5]=s.board[row*8+7];s.board[row*8+7]=null}else{s.board[row*8+3]=s.board[row*8];s.board[row*8]=null}}if(m.special==='promote')s.board[m.to]=pieceFor('q',c);if(m.special==='double')s.ep=i2sq((m.from+m.to)/2);if(p.toLowerCase()==='k')s.castling=s.castling.replace(c==='w'?'K':'k','').replace(c==='w'?'Q':'q','');const revoke={0:'q',7:'k',56:'Q',63:'K'};if(p.toLowerCase()==='r'&&revoke[m.from])s.castling=s.castling.replace(revoke[m.from],'');if(taken&&taken.toLowerCase()==='r'&&revoke[m.to])s.castling=s.castling.replace(revoke[m.to],'');return s;}
function legal(state,from){return pseudo(state,from).filter(m=>{const next=apply(state,m);return !checked(next,color(state.board[from]))});}
function allLegal(state){const out=[];for(let i=0;i<64;i++)if(state.board[i]&&color(state.board[i])===state.turn)out.push(...legal(state,i));return out;}
function moveUci(m){return i2sq(m.from)+i2sq(m.to)+(m.special==='promote'?'q':'')}

return {parseFen,sq2i,i2sq,color,other,checked,legal,allLegal,apply,moveUci};
})();

/* V31: enhanced practice studios integrated into V29, with authored answers and honest assessment. */
let v30Active=null,v30Timers=[],v30PianoRound=null;
let v31TempoScale=1;
function v31EffectiveBpm(a){return Math.round(V30_PIANO[a.index].bpm*v31TempoScale)}
const V30_STORE='v30:practice';
const V30_META={spanish:{name:'Spanish',title:'Have a real conversation',label:'Practice a conversation',desc:'Talk through cafés, plans, mistakes, disagreements and the subjunctive. Reply to what someone actually says.'},piano:{name:'Piano',title:'Play with a steady pulse',label:'Rhythm & melody studio',desc:'A real keyboard, count-in, sound, and note-by-note timing feedback—not just pressing the right keys.'},chess:{name:'Chess',title:'Calculate before you move',label:'Tactics studio',desc:'Make legal moves on a real board. Learn checks, threats, pins, forks, and forcing follow-ups.'}};
const v30Dataset=s=>({spanish:V30_SPANISH,piano:V30_PIANO,chess:V30_CHESS})[s]||[];
function v30Esc(x){return h(String(x??''))}
function v30SaveRecord(s,i,independent){const all=safeStore.get(V30_STORE,{})||{},k=s+':'+i,old=all[k]||{};all[k]={attempts:(old.attempts||0)+1,seen:true,independent:!!(old.independent||independent),at:Date.now(),reviewAt:Date.now()+86400000*(independent?3:1)};safeStore.set(V30_STORE,all)}
function v30Records(){return safeStore.get(V30_STORE,{})||{}}
function v30Summary(s){let rows=v30Dataset(s),r=v30Records();return {total:rows.length,done:rows.filter((_,i)=>r[s+':'+i]?.independent).length,seen:rows.filter((_,i)=>r[s+':'+i]?.seen).length}}
function v30First(s){const rows=v30Dataset(s),r=v30Records(),i=rows.findIndex((_,j)=>!r[s+':'+j]?.independent);return i<0?0:i}
function v30ClearTimers(){for(const timer of v30Timers)clearTimeout(timer);v30Timers=[]}
function v30Later(fn,ms){const id=setTimeout(fn,ms);v30Timers.push(id);return id}
function v30Back(){v30ClearTimers();v30Active=null;v30PianoRound=null;document.body.classList.remove('v30-immersive');showHome()}
function v30Open(s,i=null){if(!READY||!V30_META[s])return;v30ClearTimers();const x=Math.min(v30Dataset(s).length-1,Math.max(0,i===null?v30First(s):Number(i)||0));document.body.classList.add('v30-immersive');v30Active={subject:s,index:x,turn:0,failed:false,assisted:false,seenAnswer:false,history:[],selected:null,stage:0,lines:[],state:null,round:0,firstClean:false};v23Active=null;v21TrailActive=false;v27Active=null;activePath='';subject=s;try{drawMenu();updateTrail()}catch(e){}v30Render();}
function v30Shell(body){const a=v30Active,s=a.subject,d=v30Dataset(s)[a.index],met=v30Summary(s),m=V30_META[s];document.body.classList.remove('v24-focus');$('pageTitle').textContent=m.name+' · '+d.title;$('status').textContent=`${m.label} · ${met.done}/${met.total} independently completed`;

Ask tutor</button></header><div class="v30-grid"><main class="v30-main"><p class="v30-eyebrow">${a.index+1} OF ${v30Dataset(s).length} · ${v30Esc(d.skill)}</p><h1>${v30Esc(d.title)}</h1><div id="v30MainActivity">${body}</div><div class="v30-footnav"><button id="v30Prev" type="button" ${a.index===0?'disabled':''}>← Previous</button><button id="v30Next" type="button" ${a.index===v30Dataset(s).length-1?'disabled':''}>Next lesson →</button></div></main><aside class="v30-side"><h2>Your ${v30Esc(m.name)} pathway</h2><p>${met.done} mastered without help · ${met.seen} explored · ${met.total} lessons</p><div class="v30-list">${side}</div><p>✓ Independently demonstrated. ○ Explored with help. Revealing an answer is always allowed.</p></aside></div></div>`;
$('v30Home').onclick=v30Back;$('v30Curriculum').onclick=()=>v27Open(s);$('v31TutorTop').onclick=v30Tutor;$('v30Prev').onclick=()=>v30Open(s,a.index-1);$('v30Next').onclick=()=>v30Open(s,a.index+1);$('screen').querySelectorAll('[data-v30-choose]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v30Open(s,+b.dataset.v30Choose));
window.scrollTo?.({top:0,behavior:'instant'});
}

function v30Tutor(){if(typeof v28Open==='function')v28Open('Can you explain this part?')}
function v30Listen(s,locale='es-MX'){try{if(!window.speechSynthesis)throw Error('Speech synthesis not supported');speechSynthesis.cancel();const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(s);u.lang=locale;u.rate=.83;speechSynthesis.speak(u)}catch(e){v30Feedback('Spoken audio is not supported in this browser. The dialogue is fully available in text.')}}
function v30SpanishRender(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_SPANISH[a.index],t=d.turns[a.turn],history=a.history.map(h=>`<div class="v30-bubble"><small>YOU SAID</small><strong>${v30Esc(h)}</strong></div>`).join('');

More than one Spanish sentence may work.</div><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30Hint" type="button">A small clue</button><button id="v30Reveal" type="button">Show answer + why</button><button id="v30Ask" type="button">Ask tutor ↗</button></div><div class="v30-model" id="v30Model" hidden></div><div id="v30ContinueArea" class="v30-finish" hidden></div>`);
$('v30HearThem').onclick=()=>v30Listen(t.other);$('v30Submit').onclick=v30SpanishCheck;$('v30Input').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')v30SpanishCheck()};$('v30Hint').onclick=v30CommonHelp;$('v30Reveal').onclick=()=>v30SpanishReveal();$('v30Ask').onclick=v30Tutor;$('v30Input').focus();}
function v30Norm(s){return v27Norm(s).replace(/['’]/g,'').replace(/[()]/g,'').trim()}
function v30SpanishCheck(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_SPANISH[a.index],t=d.turns[a.turn],input=$('v30Input')?.value.trim()||'';if(!input)return;
const accepted=[t.a,...(t.also||[])].some(x=>v30Norm(x)===v30Norm(input));if(!accepted){a.failed=true;const actual=v30Norm(input),expected=v30Norm(t.a),words=expected.split(' ').filter(w=>w.length>=4),missing=words.filter(w=>!actual.split(' ').includes(w));let why='That might be another way to say it. Compare your wording with the example.';
if(/\b(llamas|llaman)\b/.test(actual)&&/\bllames\b/.test(expected))why='You used an indicative form. After espero que or quiero que, try the subjunctive: llames.';
else if(/\b(soy|es)\b/.test(actual)&&/\b(estoy|esta)\b/.test(expected))why='This sentence describes a state or location. Try estar rather than ser.';
else if(missing.length>0)why=`Look for the phrase “${missing[0]}” in this situation. ${t.clue}`;
v30Feedback(why+' This offline checker knows model replies, not every valid Spanish sentence. Unrecognized does NOT mean wrong.','try');v31Unrecognized(input,t);try{v29LastWrong={id:v29ID(v28Context()),value:input,at:Date.now()}}catch(_){}return;}
let alt=$('v31Unrecognized');if(alt)alt.remove();a.history.push(input);v30Feedback('That works. '+t.why,'good');const done=$('v30ContinueArea');done.hidden=false;done.innerHTML=`<strong>Good reply.</strong><p>${v30Esc(t.why)}</p><button type="button" id="v30Advance">${a.turn===d.turns.length-1?'Finish this conversation →':'Hear what they say next →'}</button>`;$('v30Submit').disabled=true;$('v30Input').disabled=true;$('v30Advance').onclick=()=>v30SpanishAdvance();}
function v31Unrecognized(input,t){let old=$('v31Unrecognized');if(old)old.remove();let feedback=$('v30Feedback');if(!feedback)return;let el=document.createElement('section');el.id='v31Unrecognized';el.className='v31-compare';el.innerHTML=`<b>Compare your wording</b><p><strong>You wrote:</strong> ${v30Esc(input)}</p><p><strong>One verified example:</strong> ${v30Esc(t.a)}</p><p>${v30Esc(t.why)}</p><p>Your sentence may be equally good. The offline exercise cannot reliably judge every paraphrase; you can revise, ask the tutor, or continue with this answer as assisted practice.</p><button type="button" id="v31AcceptAlternative">Continue with my reply (assisted)</button>`;feedback.after(el);$('v31AcceptAlternative').onclick=()=>{const a=v30Active;a.assisted=true;a.history.push(input);v30SpanishAdvance()}}
function v30SpanishReveal(){let a=v30Active,t=V30_SPANISH[a.index].turns[a.turn];a.assisted=true;a.seenAnswer=true;let box=$('v30Model');box.hidden=false;box.innerHTML=`<small>MODEL REPLY</small><strong>${v30Esc(t.a)}</strong><p>${v30Esc(t.why)}</p><button id="v30HearAnswer" type="button">▶ Hear the reply</button><p>Other answers can be valid; this is one verified model.</p><button type="button" id="v30UseModel">Continue using the model →</button>`;$('v30HearAnswer').onclick=()=>v30Listen(t.a);$('v30UseModel').onclick=()=>{a.history.push(t.a);v30SpanishAdvance()};v30Feedback('Take a moment to see how the reply is built. The model counts as studying, not independent mastery.','hint')}
function v30SpanishAdvance(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_SPANISH[a.index];if(a.turn<d.turns.length-1){a.turn++;v30SpanishRender();return}const independent=!a.assisted&&!a.failed;v30SaveRecord('spanish',a.index,independent);v30SpanishFinished(independent)}
function v30SpanishFinished(independent){let a=v30Active;v30Shell(`<div class="v30-done"><span>${independent?'✓ INDEPENDENT':'○ PRACTICED WITH SUPPORT'}</span><h2>${independent?'You handled the full conversation.':'You finished the conversation.'}</h2><p>${independent?'All three replies matched the verified examples on your first attempt, without help.':'Now try that exchange again without looking.'}</p><div class="v30-actions"><button type="button" id="v30ReplayScene">Try again</button><button type="button" id="v30NextScene">${a.index===V30_SPANISH.length-1?'Review from the beginning':'Next conversation →'}</button></div></div>`);$('v30ReplayScene').onclick=()=>v30Open('spanish',a.index);$('v30NextScene').onclick=()=>v30Open('spanish',(a.index+1)%V30_SPANISH.length)}
function v30Keyboard(){const names=['C4','D4','E4','F4','G4','A4','B4','C5','D5','E5'];let out='<div class="v30-keyboard" role="group" aria-label="Playable piano keyboard from middle C to E5">';for(const [i,n] of names.entries())out+=`<button type="button" class="v30-whitekey" data-v30-note="${n}" aria-label="Play ${n}"><span>${n}</span></button>`;for(const [i,n] of names.entries()){if(i===names.length-1||!['C','D','F','G','A'].includes(n[0]))continue;out+=`<button type="button" class="v30-blackkey" data-v30-note="${n[0]}#${n.slice(1)}" style="left:${(i+1)/names.length*100}%" aria-label="Play ${n[0]} sharp ${n.slice(1)}">♯</button>`}return out+'</div>'}
function v30Staff(notes){
 // Treble clef: bottom line E4, every successive line/space is one letter-name step.
 const places={C4:128,D4:118,E4:108,F4:98,G4:88,A4:78,B4:68,C5:58,D5:48,E5:38};
 const x=beat=>100+beat*145;
 return `<figure class="v30-staff-figure"><figcaption>How this phrase sits on a treble staff · left to right</figcaption><svg viewBox="0 0 710 170" role="img" aria-label="Staff notation for ${notes.map(([n,b])=>n+' on beat '+(b+1)).join(', ')}">${[28,48,68,88,108].map(y=>`<line x1="42" y1="${y}" x2="690" y2="${y}" stroke="#5c7562" stroke-width="1.6"/>`).join('')}<text x="50" y="23" font-family="Georgia,serif" font-size="11" fill="#4d745d">TREBLE</text>${[0,1,2,3].map(b=>`<text x="${x(b)}" y="15" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" fill="#49755c">${b+1}</text>`).join('')}${notes.map(([note,beat],i)=>{const y=places[note];if(y===undefined)return '';const xx=x(beat);return `${y>=128?`<line x1="${xx-23}" y1="128" x2="${xx+23}" y2="128" stroke="#325c43" stroke-width="1.8"/>`:''}<ellipse cx="${xx}" cy="${y}" rx="13" ry="9" transform="rotate(-20 ${xx} ${y})" fill="#205f43"/><line x1="${xx+12}" y1="${y-1}" x2="${xx+12}" y2="${y-37}" stroke="#205f43" stroke-width="2.4"/><text x="${xx}" y="${Math.max(148,y+39)}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="700" fill="#527560">${v30Esc(note)}</text>`}).join('')}</svg><p>Lines and spaces tell you which note to play; the beat numbers tell you <em>when</em>. A gap between notes can mean a held sound or a rest.</p></figure>`
}
function v30PianoScoreLabel(){const a=v30Active,d=V30_PIANO[a.index],notes=a.round?d.transfer:d.beats;return notes.map(([pitch,beat],i)=>`<div class="v30-rhythm-note" data-v30-n="${i}"><small>BEAT ${String(beat+1).replace('.5','½')}</small><b>${v30Esc(pitch)}</b></div>`).join('')}
function v30PianoRender(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_PIANO[a.index],notes=a.round?d.transfer:d.beats;
v30Shell(`<div class="v30-scene"><p><strong>${v30Esc(d.skill)}</strong> · ${v31EffectiveBpm(a)} beats per minute</p><p>${v30Esc(d.why)}</p></div><div class="v30-piano-instruction"><h2>${a.round?'ROUND 2 · The melody changes':'ROUND 1 · Feel the beat'}</h2><p>Press <strong>Start with count-in</strong>. You’ll hear <strong>four steady clicks</strong>. Play each printed note at its numbered beat. A beat marked 1½ falls between beats 1 and 2. Sound must be enabled for audio feedback.</p></div><div class="v30-rhythm" id="v30Rhythm">${v30PianoScoreLabel()}</div>${v30Staff(notes)}<div class="v31-speed"><label for="v31Tempo">Practice tempo</label><select id="v31Tempo" aria-label="Practice tempo"><option value="0.6">Slow · 60%</option><option value="0.8">Gentle · 80%</option><option value="1">Original tempo · 100%</option></select><small>Slower practice is encouraged. Independent mastery requires original tempo.</small></div><div class="v30-transport"><button type="button" id="v30Start">▶ Start with count-in</button><button type="button" id="v30Demo">▶ Hear the example</button><button type="button" id="v30Reset">Restart attempt</button><strong id="v30Beat">Ready</strong></div>${v30Keyboard()}<p class="v30-helptext">You can click the keys or use computer keys <strong>A S D F G H J K L ;</strong> from C4 upward.
