You can take as many attempts as you like.</div><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30Hint" type="button">Where do I start?</button><button id="v30Reveal" type="button">Show answer + why</button><button id="v30Ask" type="button">Ask tutor ↗</button></div><div class="v30-model" id="v30Model" hidden></div><div id="v30ContinueArea" class="v30-finish" hidden></div>`);
$('v31Tempo').value=String(v31TempoScale);$('v31Tempo').onchange=e=>{v31TempoScale=Number(e.target.value)||1;v30ClearTimers();v30PianoRender()};$('v30Start').onclick=v30PianoStart;$('v30Demo').onclick=v30PianoDemo;$('v30Reset').onclick=()=>v30PianoReset(false);$('v30Hint').onclick=()=>{a.assisted=true;v30Feedback('Find C4 immediately left of the two-black-key group. Count clicks 1, 2, 3, 4. When the next beat 1 arrives, play the first printed note.','hint')};$('v30Reveal').onclick=v30PianoReveal;$('v30Ask').onclick=v30Tutor;
$('screen').querySelectorAll('[data-v30-note]').forEach(b=>b.onpointerdown=e=>{e.preventDefault();v30PianoPress(b.dataset.v30Note)});v30PianoRound={running:false,notes,inputs:[],start:0,locked:false};}
function v30ClickSound(){try{v27Audio=v27Audio||new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();if(v27Audio.state==='suspended')v27Audio.resume();let t=v27Audio.currentTime,osc=v27Audio.createOscillator(),gain=v27Audio.createGain();osc.type='square';osc.frequency.value=870;gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.001,t);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.085,t+.003);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+.06);osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(v27Audio.destination);osc.start(t);osc.stop(t+.07)}catch(e){}}
function v30PianoReset(message=true){v30ClearTimers();let r=v30PianoRound;if(!r)return;r.running=false;r.inputs=[];r.locked=false;r.start=0;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v30-n]').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('played','bad'));if($('v30Beat'))$('v30Beat').textContent='Ready';if($('v30Start'))$('v30Start').disabled=false;if(message)v30Feedback('Reset. Press Start for four new count-in clicks.','info')}
function v30PianoStart(){const r=v30PianoRound,a=v30Active;if(!r||r.running||r.locked)return;v30PianoReset(false);let beat=60000/v31EffectiveBpm(a);r.running=true;r.start=performance.now()+4*beat;$('v30Start').disabled=true;$('v30Beat').textContent='Count in: 1 / 4';for(let b=0;b<4;b++)v30Later(()=>{if(!v30PianoRound?.running)return;v30ClickSound();$('v30Beat').textContent='Count in: '+(b+1)+' / 4'},b*beat);for(let b=0;b<5;b++)v30Later(()=>{if(!v30PianoRound?.running)return;v30ClickSound();$('v30Beat').textContent=b<4?'Play · Beat '+(b+1):'Finish the phrase'},(b+4)*beat);const latest=Math.max(...r.notes.map(([n,i])=>i));v30Later(()=>{if(!v30PianoRound?.running)return;if(r.inputs.length<r.notes.length){v30Feedback(`You played ${r.inputs.length} of ${r.notes.length} notes. Keep the beat flowing; try again at this tempo.`, 'try');r.running=false;$('v30Start').disabled=false}},(latest+5.4)*beat)}
function v30PianoPress(note){v27Sound(note);const r=v30PianoRound,a=v30Active;if(!r||!r.running||r.locked)return;let now=performance.now(),beat=60000/v31EffectiveBpm(a);if(now<r.start-beat*.23){v30Feedback('Wait for the four-click count-in. Then start on beat 1.','try');return}const i=r.inputs.length,expected=r.notes[i];if(!expected)return;if(expected[0]!==note){a.failed=true;v30Feedback('You pressed '+note+'. Look for '+expected[0]+' on the keyboard, then try again.','try');return}let delta=(now-r.start)/beat-expected[1];r.inputs.push({note,delta});let chip=document.querySelector(`[data-v30-n="${i}"]`);if(chip)chip.classList.add(Math.abs(delta)>.4?'bad':'played');if(r.inputs.length===r.notes.length){r.running=false;r.locked=true;v30ClearTimers();let max=Math.max(...r.inputs.map(x=>Math.abs(x.delta))),mean=r.inputs.reduce((t,x)=>t+Math.abs(x.delta),0)/r.inputs.length,limit=r.notes.some((x,j)=>j&&x[1]-r.notes[j-1][1]<=.5)?.30:.40;let accurate=max<=limit&&mean<=.24; if(!accurate)a.failed=true;v30PianoFinishRound(accurate,max,mean);}else v30Feedback(`${r.inputs.length} of ${r.notes.length} correct pitches. Keep your pulse steady.`,'good')}
function v30PianoDemo(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_PIANO[a.index],notes=a.round?d.transfer:d.beats;a.assisted=true;v30PianoReset(false);let ms=60000/v31EffectiveBpm(a);notes.forEach(([n,b])=>v30Later(()=>v27Sound(n),b*ms));v30Feedback('Listen to the note spacing. Then press Start to play it. Hearing the model makes this an assisted attempt.','hint')}
function v30PianoReveal(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_PIANO[a.index],notes=a.round?d.transfer:d.beats;a.assisted=true;v30PianoReset(false);let box=$('v30Model');box.hidden=false;box.innerHTML=`<small>NOTES AND WHEN TO PLAY THEM</small><strong>${notes.map(([n,b])=>v30Esc(n)+' (beat '+(b+1)+')').join(' · ')}</strong><p>${v30Esc(d.why)}</p><button type="button" id="v30HearModel">▶ Listen once</button>`;$('v30HearModel').onclick=v30PianoDemo;v30Feedback('Here is the model. You can try it immediately; this round will be recorded as assisted.','hint')}
function v30PianoFinishRound(accurate,max,mean){const a=v30Active;v30Feedback(accurate?`All notes correct. Timing: average ${(mean*100).toFixed(0)}% of a beat off; worst ${(max*100).toFixed(0)}%.`:`Pitches correct, but the timing was uneven. Your largest deviation was ${(max*100).toFixed(0)}% of a beat. Replay more slowly, or try again.`,accurate?'good':'try');const finish=$('v30ContinueArea');finish.hidden=false;finish.innerHTML=`<strong>${accurate?'The phrase landed.':'The pitches landed—now steady the rhythm.'}</strong><p>${accurate?'Great. A different phrase is the real test.':'You can retry for a cleaner rhythm or move on with practice recorded.'}</p><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30PianoRepeat">Try again</button><button id="v30PianoForward">${a.round?'Finish this lesson →':'Try the new phrase →'}</button></div>`;$('v30PianoRepeat').onclick=()=>{a.failed=true;v30PianoReset();finish.hidden=true};$('v30PianoForward').onclick=()=>{if(!a.round){a.firstClean=accurate&&!a.failed&&!a.assisted&&v31TempoScale===1;a.round=1;v30PianoRender();}else{const independent=a.firstClean&&accurate&&!a.failed&&!a.assisted&&v31TempoScale===1;v30SaveRecord('piano',a.index,independent);v30PianoFinished(independent)}}}
function v30PianoFinished(independent){let a=v30Active;v30Shell(`<div class="v30-done"><span>${independent?'✓ INDEPENDENT RHYTHM':'○ PRACTICE COMPLETE'}</span><h2>${independent?'Two phrases, played in time.':'You worked through both phrases.'}</h2><p>${independent?'Your pitches and beat placement passed both rounds without a reveal.':'Keep practicing at an easier tempo if necessary. This lesson is not yet marked independent.'}</p><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30Again">Practice again</button><button id="v30Forward">Next pattern →</button></div></div>`);$('v30Again').onclick=()=>v30Open('piano',a.index);$('v30Forward').onclick=()=>v30Open('piano',(a.index+1)%V30_PIANO.length)}
function v30ChessValidate(){let errs=[];for(const [i,d] of V30_CHESS.entries()){let st=V30_CHESS_CORE.parseFen(d.fen);for(let j=0;j<d.line.length;j++){const move=V30_CHESS_CORE.allLegal(st).find(m=>V30_CHESS_CORE.moveUci(m)===d.line[j]);if(!move){errs.push(i+':move:'+d.line[j]);break}st=V30_CHESS_CORE.apply(st,move);if(j<d.replies.length){const rep=V30_CHESS_CORE.allLegal(st).find(m=>V30_CHESS_CORE.moveUci(m)===d.replies[j]);if(!rep){errs.push(i+':reply:'+d.replies[j]);break}st=V30_CHESS_CORE.apply(st,rep)}}}return errs}
function v30ChessBegin(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_CHESS[a.index];a.stage=0;a.selected=null;a.lines=[];a.state=V30_CHESS_CORE.parseFen(d.fen);v30ChessRender()}
function v30ChessBoard(){let s=v30Active.state,core=V30_CHESS_CORE,p={K:'♔',Q:'♕',R:'♖',B:'♗',N:'♘',P:'♙',k:'♚',q:'♛',r:'♜',b:'♝',n:'♞',p:'♟︎'},valid=a=>core.legal(s,a);let targets=v30Active.selected===null?[]:valid(v30Active.selected).map(m=>m.to);return `<div class="v30-board" role="group" aria-label="Chess board, White at the bottom">${s.board.map((piece,i)=>`<button type="button" class="v30-sq ${((Math.floor(i/8)+i%8)%2)?'dark':'light'} ${v30Active.selected===i?'selected':''} ${targets.includes(i)?'target':''}" data-v30-sq="${i}" aria-label="${core.i2sq(i)} ${piece||'empty'}"><span class="${piece&&piece===piece.toUpperCase()?'whitepiece':'blackpiece'}">${p[piece]||''}</span>${i%8===0?`<small class="rank">${8-Math.floor(i/8)}</small>`:''}${Math.floor(i/8)===7?`<small class="file">${'abcdefgh'[i%8]}</small>`:''}</button>`).join('')}</div>`}
function v30ChessRender(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_CHESS[a.index],turn=a.stage,replyText=a.lines.length?a.lines.join(' → '):'No moves made yet';v30Shell(`<div class="v30-scene"><p><strong>${v30Esc(d.skill)}</strong></p><p>${v30Esc(d.prompt)}</p></div><div class="v30-chess-layout"><div id="v30BoardArea">${v30ChessBoard()}</div><div class="v30-chess-instructions"><strong>${turn?`YOUR FOLLOW-UP · MOVE ${turn+1}`:'YOUR MOVE · THINK FIRST'}</strong><p>${turn?'Black has replied. What can you do now? Trace your knight’s targets before tapping.':'Before touching a piece, look for checks, captures and immediate threats.'}</p><p>Moves so far: <span id="v30ChessLine">${v30Esc(replyText)}</span></p><div id="v30Feedback" class="v30-result" role="status" data-kind="info">Tap one of your pieces, then a highlighted legal square. No illegal moves accepted.</div><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30Hint" type="button">One clue</button><button id="v30Reveal" type="button">Show move + why</button><button id="v30Ask" type="button">Ask tutor ↗</button></div><div class="v30-model" id="v30Model" hidden></div><div class="v30-finish" id="v30ContinueArea" hidden></div></div></div>`);
$('v30Hint').onclick=()=>{a.assisted=true;v30Feedback(d.hint,'hint')};$('v30Reveal').onclick=v30ChessReveal;$('v30Ask').onclick=v30Tutor;$('v30BoardArea').querySelectorAll('[data-v30-sq]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v30ChessSquare(+b.dataset.v30Sq));}
function v30ChessSquare(index){let a=v30Active,c=V30_CHESS_CORE,s=a.state;if(!s)return;let pc=s.board[index],isOwn=pc&&c.color(pc)===s.turn;if(isOwn){a.selected=index;v30ChessUpdateBoard();v30Feedback('Highlighted squares are legal destinations. Which one achieves your goal?','info');return}if(a.selected===null){v30Feedback('Choose one of your pieces first.','try');return}let options=c.legal(s,a.selected),move=options.find(x=>x.to===index);if(!move){a.failed=true;a.selected=null;v30ChessUpdateBoard();v30Feedback('That destination is not legal. Check how your piece moves and whether your king would remain in check.','try');return}let u=c.moveUci(move),d=V30_CHESS[a.index],want=d.line[a.stage];if(u!==want){a.failed=true;a.selected=null;v30ChessUpdateBoard();v30Feedback('That move is legal, but it does not solve the specific challenge. '+d.hint,'try');return}a.state=c.apply(a.state,move);a.lines.push(u);a.selected=null;if(a.stage<d.replies.length){const next=d.replies[a.stage],opp=c.allLegal(a.state).find(m=>c.moveUci(m)===next);if(opp){a.state=c.apply(a.state,opp);a.lines.push(next);}a.stage++;v30ChessRender();v30Feedback('Correct first move. In this example Black plays '+next+'. Find the follow-up.','good');return}v30ChessSolved()}
function v30ChessUpdateBoard(){const el=$('v30BoardArea');if(!el)return;el.innerHTML=v30ChessBoard();el.querySelectorAll('[data-v30-sq]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v30ChessSquare(+b.dataset.v30Sq));}
function v30ChessReveal(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_CHESS[a.index],move=d.line[a.stage];a.assisted=true;let m=$('v30Model');m.hidden=false;m.innerHTML=`<small>THE WORKED MOVE</small><strong>${v30Esc(move.slice(0,2)+' → '+move.slice(2))}</strong><p>${v30Esc(d.why)}</p><button type="button" id="v30ChessShowIt">Play this move for me →</button>`;$('v30ChessShowIt').onclick=()=>{let found=V30_CHESS_CORE.allLegal(a.state).find(x=>V30_CHESS_CORE.moveUci(x)===move);if(found){a.selected=found.from;v30ChessSquare(found.to)}};v30Feedback('This is a verified move in the stored chess position. Revealing it makes the attempt assisted.','hint')}
function v30ChessSolved(){let a=v30Active,d=V30_CHESS[a.index],independent=!a.failed&&!a.assisted;v30SaveRecord('chess',a.index,independent);v30ChessRender();let f=$('v30ContinueArea');f.hidden=false;f.innerHTML=`<strong>${independent?'✓ Solved independently.':'○ Position explored.'}</strong><p>${v30Esc(d.why)}</p><div class="v30-actions"><button id="v30ChessAgain">Replay position</button><button id="v30ChessForward">Next puzzle →</button></div>`;$('v30ChessAgain').onclick=()=>v30Open('chess',a.index);$('v30ChessForward').onclick=()=>v30Open('chess',(a.index+1)%V30_CHESS.length);$('v30Hint').disabled=true;$('v30Reveal').disabled=true;v30Feedback(independent?'You calculated the right line without help.':'Good to see the complete idea. Try the puzzle again independently when ready.','good')}
function v30Context(){const a=v30Active;if(!a)return null;const d=v30Dataset(a.subject)[a.index],mode={spanish:'guided conversation',piano:'timed piano practice',chess:'calculation puzzle'}[a.subject];let question='',model='',clue='',background='',explanation='',round=0;if(a.subject==='spanish'){const t=d.turns[a.turn]||d.turns[0];question=t.ask;model=t.a;clue=t.clue;explanation=t.why;background=`Situation: ${d.setting}. Another person said: ${t.other} (${t.en}). Learner must reply. Recognized alternatives: ${(t.also||[]).join(' | ')}`;round=a.turn+1}
if(a.subject==='piano'){let seq=a.round?d.transfer:d.beats;question='Play the printed melody on the beat at '+d.bpm+' BPM';model=seq.map(([n,b])=>`${n} on beat ${b+1}`).join(', ');clue='Locate middle C to the left of the two black keys. Count four metronome clicks before playing.';explanation=d.why;background='Piano exercise checks both pitch and relative timing. Metronome provides four count-in clicks.';round=a.round+1}
if(a.subject==='chess'){question=d.prompt+(a.stage?' Black replied. Find the follow-up.':'');model=d.line[a.stage]||d.line.at(-1);clue=d.hint;explanation=d.why;background='Current FEN: '+d.fen+'; moves played: '+a.lines.join(' ')+'; only suggest moves that are legal in this position.';round=a.stage+1}
return {subject:a.subject,mode,title:d.title,question,modelAnswer:model,explanation,clue,background,round,selectedText:''};}
function v31NextItem(){const subjects=['spanish','piano','chess'],saves=v30Records(),now=Date.now(),due=[];for(const s of subjects){v30Dataset(s).forEach((d,i)=>{let r=saves[s+':'+i];if(r?.seen&&r.reviewAt<=now)due.push({s,i,at:r.reviewAt,mode:'review'});})}due.sort((a,b)=>a.at-b.at);if(due.length)return due[0];for(const s of subjects){const i=v30Dataset(s).findIndex((d,j)=>!saves[s+':'+j]?.independent);if(i>=0)return {s,i,mode:'new'}}return {s:'spanish',i:0,mode:'refresh'};}
function v31InsertReview(box){const x=v31NextItem(),d=v30Dataset(x.s)[x.i],review=document.createElement('div');review.className='v31-review';review.innerHTML=`<div><b>${x.mode==='review'?'Ready for review':x.mode==='new'?'Your next small win':'Keep your skills fresh'}</b><p>${v30Esc(V30_META[x.s].name)} · ${v30Esc(d.title)} · ${x.mode==='review'?'See what you still remember without hints.':'One exercise is enough to get going.'}</p></div><button type="button" id="v31StartReview">${x.mode==='review'?'Review now':'Start here'} →</button>`;box.querySelector('.v30-home-head').after(review);$('v31StartReview').onclick=()=>v30Open(x.s,x.i)}

Average speed = (16 − 4) ÷ (4 − 1) = 4 miles/hour.","questions":[{"q":"A plant grows from 8 cm on day 2 to 20 cm on day 6. function v32Summary(s){const records=v32Records(),data=V32_LESSONS[s];return {done:data.filter((_,i)=>records[s+':'+i]?.independent).length,seen:data.filter((_,i)=>records[s+':'+i]?.seen).length,total:data.length}}
function v32Next(s){const rows=V32_LESSONS[s],saved=v32Records(),now=Date.now();const due=rows.findIndex((_,i)=>saved[s+':'+i]?.seen&&saved[s+':'+i]?.reviewAt<=now);if(due>=0)return due;const next=rows.findIndex((_,i)=>!saved[s+':'+i]?.independent);return next<0?0:next}
function v32Save(s,i,independent){const all=v32Records(),key=s+':'+i,old=all[key]||{};all[key]={attempts:(old.attempts||0)+1,seen:true,independent:!!(old.independent||independent),lastIndependent:independent,at:Date.now(),reviewAt:Date.now()+(independent?3:1)*86400000};safeStore.set(V32_STORE,all)}
function v32Esc(x){return v30Esc(x)}

function v32Choose(index){const a=v32Active;if(!a||a.answered[a.round])return;const q=V32_LESSONS[a.subject][a.index].questions[a.round];const buttons=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-v32-answer]')];const chosen=q.options[index];if(index===q.answer){a.answered[a.round]=true;a.results.push(true);buttons.forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;b.classList.toggle('v32-correct',+b.dataset.v32Answer===q.answer)});$('v32Feedback').textContent='Yes. /* V33: practice from memory with spaced scheduling. Independent from V32 workshop records. */
const V33_KEY='studytable:v33:recall';
const v33Read=()=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(V33_KEY)||'{}')}catch{return {}}};

let v33Queue=[],v33Position=0,v33Showing=false;
function v33Candidates(){if(typeof V32_LESSONS==='undefined')return [];return Object.entries(V32_LESSONS).flatMap(([subject,lessons])=>lessons.flatMap((lesson,index)=>lesson.questions.map((q,round)=>({subject,index,round,q,lesson,id:subject+':'+index+':'+round}))));}
function v33Start(){const now=Date.now(),db=v33Read(),all=v33Candidates();v33Queue=all.filter(c=>!db[c.id]||db[c.id].due<=now).sort((a,b)=>(db[a.id]?.due||0)-(db[b.id]?.due||0)).slice(0,12);if(!v33Queue.length){v33Queue=all.sort((a,b)=>(db[a.id]?.due||0)-(db[b.id]?.due||0)).slice(0,6)}v33Position=0;v33Showing=false;v33Render()}
function v33Render(){let host=document.getElementById('v33Overlay');if(!host){host=document.createElement('div');host.id='v33Overlay';document.body.append(host)}const c=v33Queue[v33Position];if(!c){host.innerHTML='<div class="v33-dialog"><button class="v33-exit" id="v33Exit">Close ×</button><h2>Review complete</h2><p>You practiced recalling answers rather than simply rereading them. Come back tomorrow for another round.</p><button id="v33Again">Another round</button></div>';host.hidden=false;document.getElementById('v33Exit').onclick=v33Close;document.getElementById('v33Again').onclick=v33Start;return}const meta=V32_META[c.subject];host.innerHTML=`<div class="v33-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Memory review"><button class="v33-exit" id="v33Exit">Close ×</button><p class="v33-eyebrow">MEMORY REVIEW · ${v33Position+1} OF ${v33Queue.length} · ${v32Esc(meta?.name||c.subject)}</p><h2>${v32Esc(c.q.q)}</h2><div class="v33-answers">${c.q.options.map((a,i)=>`<button data-v33-option="${i}">${v32Esc(a)}</button>`).join('')}</div><div id="v33Result" aria-live="polite"></div><button id="v33Reveal">Show answer and explanation</button></div>`;host.hidden=false;document.getElementById('v33Exit').onclick=v33Close;document.getElementById('v33Reveal').onclick=()=>v33Answer(-1);host.querySelectorAll('[data-v33-option]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v33Answer(+b.dataset.v33Option));}
function v33Answer(choice){if(v33Showing)return;v33Showing=true;const c=v33Queue[v33Position],correct=choice===c.q.answer,db=v33Read(),prev=db[c.id]||{level:0};const level=correct?Math.min(5,prev.level+1):0;const days=[1,2,4,7,14,30][level];db[c.id]={level,due:Date.now()+days*86400000,lastCorrect:correct};v33Write(db);const el=document.getElementById('v33Result');el.innerHTML=`<div class="v33-feedback"><strong>${correct?'Correct — remembered.':choice<0?'Here is the answer.':'Not yet — here is the answer.'}</strong><p><b>${v32Esc(c.q.options[c.q.answer])}</b> — ${v32Esc(c.q.why)}</p><small>${correct?'Scheduled again in '+days+' day(s).':'Scheduled for another try tomorrow.'}</small></div><button id="v33Continue">${v33Position+1===v33Queue.length?'Finish review':'Next question →'}</button>`;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v33-option]').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;if(+b.dataset.v33Option===c.q.answer)b.classList.add('v33-right')});document.getElementById('v33Reveal').hidden=true;document.getElementById('v33Continue').onclick=()=>{v33Position++;v33Showing=false;v33Render()}}

let v34State=null,v34AudioTimers=[],v34KeyLabels=true;
const v34Escape=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
const v34N=s=>String(s||'').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
function v34Read(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('studytable:v34:studio')||'{}')}catch{return {}}}
function v34Save(key,complete,independent=false){try{const r=v34Read();r[key]={attempts:(r[key]?.attempts||0)+1,completed:!!complete||!!r[key]?.completed,independent:!!independent||!!r[key]?.independent,updated:Date.now()};localStorage.setItem('studytable:v34:studio',JSON.stringify(r))}catch{}}
function v34ClearAudio(){v34AudioTimers.forEach(t=>clearTimeout(t));v34AudioTimers=[]}
function v34Play(notes,pace=550){v34ClearAudio();notes.forEach((n,i)=>v34AudioTimers.push(setTimeout(()=>{if(v34State?.subject==='piano')v27Sound(n)},i*pace)))}
function v34Host(){let el=document.getElementById('v34Studio');if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='v34Studio';el.hidden=true;el.innerHTML='<div class="v34-shell" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Learning studios"><div id="v34Content"></div></div>';document.body.append(el)}return el}
function v34Open(subject=null,index=0){v34ClearAudio();v34State={subject:subject||'home',index,step:0,errors:0,usedHelp:false,showing:false,history:[],pianoProgress:0,pianoShowGuide:index<3};v34Host().hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('v34-active');v34Render();}
function v34Close(){if(v34Recognition){try{v34Recognition.abort()}catch{}}v34ClearAudio();v34Host().hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('v34-active');v34State=null}
function v34Frame(content){const el=document.getElementById('v34Content');el.innerHTML=`<header class="v34-head"><div><span class="v34-kicker">STUDY TABLE · GUIDED STUDIOS</span><h2>Get good at the thing.</h2></div><button id="v34Close" type="button" aria-label="Close learning studios">Close ×</button></header><nav class="v34-tabs" aria-label="Choose a studio"><button data-v34-tab="home" ${v34State.subject==='home'?'aria-current="page"':''}>All studios</button><button data-v34-tab="spanish" ${v34State.subject==='spanish'?'aria-current="page"':''}>Spanish</button><button data-v34-tab="piano" ${v34State.subject==='piano'?'aria-current="page"':''}>Piano</button><button data-v34-tab="chess" ${v34State.subject==='chess'?'aria-current="page"':''}>Chess</button></nav><div class="v34-body">${content}</div>`;el.querySelector('#v34Close').onclick=v34Close;el.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-tab]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v34Open(b.dataset.v34Tab));}
function v34Render(){if(!v34State)return;switch(v34State.subject){case'spanish':v34Spanish();break;case'piano':v34Piano();break;case'chess':v34Chess();break;default:v34Home()}}
function v34Home(){const scores=v34Read();const groups=[['spanish','Hold a real conversation',V34_SPANISH,'From ordering coffee to telling stories in three past tenses. You speak every turn.'],['piano','Actually play the keyboard',V34_PIANO,'Hear a melody, find each key and play at your own pace—no timer at first.'],['chess','Understand the move',V30_CHESS,'Look at real positions, name the idea, then play the actual tactic.']];v34Frame(`<div class="v34-welcome"><h3>Choose something you’ll be able to do in five minutes.</h3><p>Try it yourself, get one clue, or see the model answer and why it works. The complete original courses remain one click away.</p></div><div class="v34-home-grid">${groups.map(([s,title,lessons,desc])=>`<article class="v34-course"><span>${s.toUpperCase()} · ${lessons.length} sessions</span><h3>${title}</h3><p>${desc}</p><small>${lessons.filter((_,i)=>scores[s+':'+i]?.completed).length} / ${lessons.length} completed · ${lessons.filter((_,i)=>scores[s+':'+i]?.independent).length} independent</small><button type="button" data-v34-start="${s}">Start ${s} →</button></article>`).join('')}</div><p class="v34-note">Your completed sessions are saved on this device. The offline coach can explain verified lesson content; a secure online AI tutor is optional when the backend is enabled.</p>`);document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-start]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v34Open(b.dataset.v34Start));}
function v34Toolbar(subject,arr){const a=v34State,completed=arr.filter((_,i)=>v34Read()[subject+':'+i]?.completed).length;return `<div class="v34-toolbar"><label for="v34Choose">Session</label><select id="v34Choose">${arr.map((d,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${i===a.index?'selected':''}>${i+1}. ${v34Escape(d.title)}</option>`).join('')}</select><span>${completed}/${arr.length} completed</span><button type="button" id="v34Tutor">Ask coach about this ↗</button></div>`}
function v34WireToolbar(){document.getElementById('v34Choose').onchange=e=>v34Open(v34State.subject,Number(e.target.value));document.getElementById('v34Tutor').onclick=()=>{if(typeof v28Open==='function'){v28Open('Can you help me understand this exact step?');}else window.studyTableV90?.notice('The guided answers are available on this page.')}}
function v34Spanish(){const a=v34State,d=V34_SPANISH[a.index],t=d.steps[a.step];let previous=a.history.map((h,i)=>`<div class="v34-said"><small>YOU · ${i+1}</small><span>${v34Escape(h)}</span></div>`).join('');v34Frame(`${v34Toolbar('spanish',V34_SPANISH)}<div class="v34-lesson"><span class="v34-level">${d.level} · CONVERSATION · ${a.step+1}/${d.steps.length}</span><h3>${v34Escape(d.title)}</h3><p class="v34-desc">${v34Escape(d.scene)}</p><div class="v34-convo"><div class="v34-other"><small>THE OTHER PERSON</small><strong>${v34Escape(t[0])}</strong><span>${v34Escape(t[1])}</span><button id="v34Listen" type="button">▶ Hear it</button></div>${previous}</div><div class="v34-prompt"><span>YOUR TURN</span><h4>${v34Escape(t[2])}</h4></div><div class="v34-entry"><input type="text" id="v34SpanishInput" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" aria-label="Your Spanish reply" placeholder="Write what you'd say in Spanish"><button id="v34SpanishCheck" type="button">Check</button></div><p class="v34-small">Offline checking recognizes the model reply and several alternatives. An unrecognized sentence is not necessarily incorrect.</p><div class="v34-actions"><button id="v34Hint" type="button">One clue</button><button id="v34Show" type="button">Show answer + why</button><button id="v34Speak" type="button">Hear my reply</button><button id="v34Dictate" type="button">🎙 Dictate in Spanish</button></div><div id="v34Feedback" class="v34-feedback" aria-live="polite"></div><button class="v34-next" id="v34Next" type="button" hidden>${a.step===d.steps.length-1?'Complete conversation →':'Next reply →'}</button></div>`);v34WireToolbar();document.getElementById('v34Listen').onclick=()=>v30Listen(t[0]);document.getElementById('v34SpanishCheck').onclick=v34SpanishCheck;document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput').onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter')v34SpanishCheck()};document.getElementById('v34Hint').onclick=()=>{a.usedHelp=true;document.getElementById('v34Feedback').innerHTML='<strong>Clue</strong><p>'+v34Escape(t[6])+'</p>'};document.getElementById('v34Show').onclick=()=>v34SpanishAnswer(false);document.getElementById('v34Speak').onclick=()=>{const v=document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput').value.trim();if(v)v30Listen(v);else document.getElementById('v34Feedback').textContent='Write your reply first, then hear it spoken.'};document.getElementById('v34Dictate').onclick=v34Dictate;document.getElementById('v34Next').onclick=v34SpanishNext;document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput').focus();}

let v34Recognition=null;
function v34Dictate(){
 const Engine=window.SpeechRecognition||window.webkitSpeechRecognition;
 if(!Engine){document.getElementById('v34Feedback').textContent='Speech input is unavailable here. You can still type your answer and use the Hear buttons.';return}
 try{if(v34Recognition)v34Recognition.abort();const r=new Engine();v34Recognition=r;r.lang='es-ES';r.interimResults=false;r.maxAlternatives=1;
  document.getElementById('v34Feedback').textContent='Listening in Spanish. Speak your reply now.';
  r.onresult=e=>{const t=e.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript||'';const input=document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput');if(input&&!input.disabled){input.value=t;input.focus()}document.getElementById('v34Feedback').textContent='I heard: '+t+'. Check or edit the transcription before submitting.'};
  r.onerror=()=>{const el=document.getElementById('v34Feedback');if(el)el.textContent='Microphone transcription is unavailable or was denied. Typing works just as well.'};
  r.start();
 }catch(e){document.getElementById('v34Feedback').textContent='Could not start speech input. Please type your reply.'}
}

function v34SpanishCheck(){const a=v34State,t=V34_SPANISH[a.index].steps[a.step],input=document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput').value.trim();if(!input)return;const options=[t[3],...(t[4]||[])],n=v34N(input);if(options.some(s=>v34N(s)===n)){a.history.push(input);v34SpanishAnswer(true);return}a.errors++;document.getElementById('v34Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>That wording isn't in this offline exercise's answer set.</strong><p>It may still be natural Spanish. Compare it with the model answer, try another version, or ask the online tutor if it is connected. This is <b>not marked wrong</b> automatically.</p><button type="button" id="v34Compare">Compare with model answer</button> <button type="button" id="v34AskWording">Ask coach about my sentence</button>`;document.getElementById('v34Compare').onclick=()=>v34SpanishAnswer(false);document.getElementById('v34AskWording').onclick=()=>v28Open(`Is my Spanish reply natural? I wrote: ${input}. The situation is: ${t[2]}. Please compare it to: ${t[3]}.`);}
function v34SpanishAnswer(verified){const a=v34State,t=V34_SPANISH[a.index].steps[a.step];if(!verified)a.usedHelp=true;if(!verified&&!a.history[a.step])a.history.push(t[3]);document.getElementById('v34Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>${verified?'That reply works.':'Model reply'}</strong><p class="v34-model">${v34Escape(t[3])}</p><p>${v34Escape(t[5])}</p><button id="v34HearModel" type="button">▶ Hear the model reply</button>`;document.getElementById('v34HearModel').onclick=()=>v30Listen(t[3]);document.getElementById('v34SpanishInput').disabled=true;document.getElementById('v34SpanishCheck').disabled=true;document.getElementById('v34Next').hidden=false;}
function v34SpanishNext(){const a=v34State,d=V34_SPANISH[a.index];if(a.step===d.steps.length-1){v34Save('spanish:'+a.index,true,!a.usedHelp&&!a.errors);v34Finish('spanish',d.title);return}a.step++;v34Spanish()}
function v34PianoKeyboard(){let notes=['C','D','E','F','G','A','B'];let whites=[];for(let o=4;o<=5;o++){for(let j=0;j<7;j++)whites.push(notes[j]+o)}whites.push('C6');const blackAfter={C:'C#',D:'D#',F:'F#',G:'G#',A:'A#'};const black=whites.map((n,i)=>({n,i,after:blackAfter[n[0]]?blackAfter[n[0]]+n.slice(1):null})).filter(x=>x.after&&x.i<14);return `<div class="v34-keyboard" role="group" aria-label="Piano keyboard with two octaves">${whites.map((n,i)=>`<button type="button" data-v34-key="${n}" aria-label="Play ${n}" class="v34-white"><span>${n}</span></button>`).join('')}${black.map(x=>`<button type="button" data-v34-key="${x.after}" aria-label="Play ${x.after}" class="v34-black" style="left:${(x.i+1)/15*100}%"><span>${x.after.replace('#','♯')}</span></button>`).join('')}</div>`}
function v34Piano(){const a=v34State,d=V34_PIANO[a.index],current=d.notes[a.pianoProgress];v34Frame(`${v34Toolbar('piano',V34_PIANO)}<div class="v34-lesson"><span class="v34-level">PIANO · ${d.skill.toUpperCase()} · ${a.index+1}/${V34_PIANO.length}</span><h3>${v34Escape(d.title)}</h3><p class="v34-desc">${v34Escape(d.tip)}</p><div class="v34-note-map"><span>Read left to right:</span><strong>${v34Escape(d.count)}</strong></div><div class="v34-score" role="group" aria-label="Notes in this melody">${d.notes.map((n,i)=>`<div data-v34-step="${i}" class="${i===a.pianoProgress?'now':''} ${i<a.pianoProgress?'played':''}"><small>${i+1}</small>${v34Escape(n.replace('#','♯'))}</div>`).join('')}</div><div class="v34-now"><span>Find and play this key</span><strong id="v34Target">${current?v34Escape(current.replace('#','♯')):'Complete!'}</strong><p id="v34KeyStatus" aria-live="polite">${a.pianoShowGuide?'Look for the outlined key.':'Find this note without a highlighted key.'} Playing a different note is safe—try again.</p></div>${v34PianoKeyboard()}<p class="v34-small">The white C immediately before each pair of black keys is your landmark. Octave 4 begins at middle C. You can play with help or hide the outlined keys to test yourself.</p><div class="v34-actions"><button type="button" id="v34PianoHear">▶ Hear the full melody</button><button type="button" id="v34PianoOne">▶ Hear next note</button><button type="button" id="v34PianoReset">Start again</button><button type="button" id="v34PianoHelp">Show me where</button><button type="button" id="v34PianoMode">${a.pianoShowGuide?'Try without highlighted keys':'Turn key guide on'}</button></div><div id="v34PianoFinish" hidden class="v34-feedback"></div></div>`);v34WireToolbar();document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v34Press(b.dataset.v34Key));document.getElementById('v34PianoHear').onclick=()=>v34Play(d.notes,650);document.getElementById('v34PianoOne').onclick=()=>v27Sound(d.notes[a.pianoProgress]||d.notes[0]);document.getElementById('v34PianoReset').onclick=()=>{a.pianoProgress=0;a.errors=0;a.usedHelp=false;v34Piano()};document.getElementById('v34PianoHelp').onclick=()=>{a.usedHelp=true;a.pianoShowGuide=true;v34PointKey(true);document.getElementById('v34PianoMode').textContent='Try without highlighted keys'};document.getElementById('v34PianoMode').onclick=()=>{a.pianoShowGuide=!a.pianoShowGuide;if(a.pianoShowGuide)a.usedHelp=true;v34PointKey(false);document.getElementById('v34PianoMode').textContent=a.pianoShowGuide?'Try without highlighted keys':'Turn key guide on'};v34PointKey(false);}
function v34PointKey(help){document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-key]').forEach(k=>k.classList.remove('v34-target'));const n=V34_PIANO[v34State.index].notes[v34State.pianoProgress],key=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-key]')].find(k=>k.dataset.v34Key===n);if(key&&v34State.pianoShowGuide)key.classList.add('v34-target');if(help)document.getElementById('v34KeyStatus').textContent='The gold-outlined key is '+n+'. Tap it to hear it and advance.';}
function v34Press(n){const a=v34State;if(a?.subject!=='piano')return;v27Sound(n);const d=V34_PIANO[a.index];if(a.pianoProgress>=d.notes.length)return;const current=d.notes[a.pianoProgress];if(n!==current){a.errors++;document.getElementById('v34KeyStatus').textContent='That was '+n+'. You need '+current+'. Try the outlined key.';return}a.pianoProgress++;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-step]').forEach((b,i)=>{b.classList.toggle('played',i<a.pianoProgress);b.classList.toggle('now',i===a.pianoProgress)});const next=d.notes[a.pianoProgress];document.getElementById('v34Target').textContent=next?next.replace('#','♯'):'Complete!';if(next){document.getElementById('v34KeyStatus').textContent=`${a.pianoProgress}/${d.notes.length} correct notes. Find ${next}.`;v34PointKey(false);}else{v34Save('piano:'+a.index,true,!a.pianoShowGuide&&!a.usedHelp&&!a.errors);document.getElementById('v34KeyStatus').textContent=!a.pianoShowGuide&&!a.usedHelp&&!a.errors?'Every note correct without highlighted keys.':'You played every note in order.';document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-key]').forEach(k=>k.classList.remove('v34-target'));const el=document.getElementById('v34PianoFinish');el.hidden=false;el.innerHTML=`<strong>${!a.pianoShowGuide&&!a.usedHelp&&!a.errors?'You played that from memory!':'You played the full phrase!'}</strong><p>${v34Escape(d.tip)}</p><button type="button" id="v34NextPiece">${a.index+1===V34_PIANO.length?'Start again':'Next piece →'}</button>`;document.getElementById('v34NextPiece').onclick=()=>v34Open('piano',(a.index+1)%V34_PIANO.length);}}
function v34ChessBoard(fen){const grid=[],pieces={K:'♔',Q:'♕',R:'♖',B:'♗',N:'♘',P:'♙',k:'♚',q:'♛',r:'♜',b:'♝',n:'♞',p:'♟'};for(const row of fen.split(' ')[0].split('/'))for(const c of row){if(/\d/.test(c))for(let x=0;x<Number(c);x++)grid.push('');else grid.push(c)}return `<div class="v34-chessboard" role="img" aria-label="Chess position with white at the bottom">${grid.map((p,i)=>`<div class="${(Math.floor(i/8)+i%8)%2?'dark':'light'}"><span class="${p&&p===p.toUpperCase()?'whitepiece':'blackpiece'}">${pieces[p]||''}</span>${i%8===0?`<small class="rank">${8-Math.floor(i/8)}</small>`:''}${i>=56?`<small class="file">${'abcdefgh'[i%8]}</small>`:''}</div>`).join('')}</div>`}
function v34Chess(){const a=v34State,d=V30_CHESS[a.index],r=V34_CHESS_REASONS[a.index];if(!d||!r){v34Open('home');return}v34Frame(`${v34Toolbar('chess',V30_CHESS)}<div class="v34-lesson"><span class="v34-level">CHESS · ${v34Escape(d.skill.toUpperCase())} · POSITION ${a.index+1}/${V30_CHESS.length}</span><h3>${v34Escape(d.title)}</h3><p class="v34-desc">${v34Escape(d.prompt)}</p><div class="v34-chess-layout">${v34ChessBoard(d.fen)}<div class="v34-chess-think"><span class="v34-kicker">BEFORE YOU MOVE</span><h4>${v34Escape(r[0])}</h4><div class="v34-reasons">${r[1].map((choice,i)=>`<button data-v34-reason="${i}" type="button">${v34Escape(choice)}</button>`).join('')}</div><div id="v34ChessFeedback" class="v34-feedback" aria-live="polite">Think about checks, captures, threats and safety. Pick your reasoning first.</div><div class="v34-actions"><button type="button" id="v34ChessClue">One clue</button><button type="button" id="v34ChessReveal">Reveal reasoning</button><button type="button" id="v34ChessPlay">Play this position on the board →</button></div></div></div></div>`);v34WireToolbar();document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-reason]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v34ChessGrade(Number(b.dataset.v34Reason)));document.getElementById('v34ChessClue').onclick=()=>{a.usedHelp=true;document.getElementById('v34ChessFeedback').textContent=d.hint};document.getElementById('v34ChessReveal').onclick=()=>v34ChessGrade(-1);document.getElementById('v34ChessPlay').onclick=()=>{const index=a.index;v34Close();v30Open('chess',index)};}
function v34ChessGrade(selected){const a=v34State,r=V34_CHESS_REASONS[a.index],d=V30_CHESS[a.index],correct=selected===r[2];if(selected!==r[2])a.errors++;if(selected<0)a.usedHelp=true;if(correct)v34Save('chess:'+a.index,true,!a.usedHelp&&!a.errors);const el=document.getElementById('v34ChessFeedback');el.innerHTML=`<strong>${correct?'Exactly.':selected<0?'How to work it out':'Not quite; here is the key idea'}</strong><p>${v34Escape(r[1][r[2]])} ${v34Escape(r[3])}</p><p><b>What to play:</b> ${v34Escape(d.line?.[0]||'Open the board to explore.')} · ${v34Escape(d.why)}</p><button type="button" id="v34ChessNext">${a.index+1===V30_CHESS.length?'First position →':'Next position →'}</button>`;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v34-reason]').forEach(b=>{b.disabled=true;if(Number(b.dataset.v34Reason)===r[2])b.classList.add('right')});document.getElementById('v34ChessNext').onclick=()=>v34Open('chess',(a.index+1)%V30_CHESS.length);}
function v34Finish(subject,title){const a=v34State;v34Frame(`${v34Toolbar(subject,subject==='spanish'?V34_SPANISH:subject==='piano'?V34_PIANO:V30_CHESS)}<section class="v34-done"><span class="v34-level">SESSION COMPLETED</span><h3>${v34Escape(title)}</h3><p>You reached the end of this session. ${a.usedHelp?'You used a worked answer or clue, which is part of learning.':'You finished without revealing the model answer.'} You can review or choose something harder next.</p><div class="v34-actions"><button id="v34Replay" type="button">Try this again</button><button id="v34Forward" type="button">Next session →</button><button id="v34All" type="button">All studios</button></div></section>`);v34WireToolbar();document.getElementById('v34Replay').onclick=()=>v34Open(subject,a.index);document.getElementById('v34Forward').onclick=()=>v34Open(subject,(a.index+1)%(subject==='spanish'?V34_SPANISH:V34_PIANO).length);document.getElementById('v34All').onclick=()=>v34Open('home');}
// The existing lesson coach gets the exact problem context when inside the new studios.
const v34PreviousContext=v28Context;
v28Context=function(...args){if(!v34State||v34State.subject==='home')return v34PreviousContext(...args);const a=v34State,subject=a.subject;if(subject==='spanish'){const d=V34_SPANISH[a.index],t=d.steps[a.step];return {subject,mode:'guided Spanish conversation',title:d.title,question:`${t[0]} (${t[1]}). ${t[2]}`,modelAnswer:t[3],explanation:t[5],clue:t[6],background:`Scene: ${d.scene}. Level: ${d.level}. Other acceptable replies: ${(t[4]||[]).join(' | ')}. The offline validator cannot recognize all valid phrasings.`,round:a.step+1,selectedText:''}}if(subject==='piano'){const d=V34_PIANO[a.index];return {subject,mode:'interactive piano keyboard',title:d.title,question:`How do I find and play ${d.notes[a.pianoProgress]||'the melody'}?`,modelAnswer:d.notes.join(' → '),explanation:d.tip,clue:'Find the outlined key; C is the white key immediately left of each group of two black keys.',background:`Progress: ${a.pianoProgress}/${d.notes.length}. ${d.skill}. First learn the notes without any timer.`,round:a.pianoProgress+1,selectedText:''}}const d=V30_CHESS[a.index],r=V34_CHESS_REASONS[a.index];return {subject,mode:'chess reasoning studio',title:d.title,question:r[0]+' '+d.prompt,modelAnswer:r[1][r[2]]+' Move: '+(d.line?.[0]||''),explanation:r[3]+' '+d.why,clue:d.hint,background:'FEN: '+d.fen+'; Choices: '+r[1].join(' | '),round:1,selectedText:''}}
const v34PreviousOffline=v28OfflineAnswer;
v28OfflineAnswer=function(question,context){if(!v34State||v34State.subject==='home')return v34PreviousOffline(question,context);const q=v34N(question);if(/(show|give|what is|tell).*(answer|solution|move)|\banswer\b/.test(q))return `${context.modelAnswer}\n\n${context.explanation}`;if(/hint|clue|stuck|start|first/.test(q))return `Try this first: ${context.clue}\n\nWhat do you notice?`;if(/why|explain|how|understand|confus|wrong|mistake/.test(q))return `${context.explanation}\n\n${context.clue}`;if(/different|another|alternative/.test(q))return `Here's the verified example: ${context.modelAnswer}. ${context.explanation}\n\nOther answers may be valid, especially in conversational Spanish. I cannot reliably grade an unfamiliar sentence offline.`;return `In “${context.title}”, the task is: ${context.question}\n\nA useful starting point: ${context.clue}\n\nYou can ask “why,” “show the answer,” or “give me a hint.” For questions beyond this lesson, see the tutor settings.`;}

const V35_SPANISH=[{"title":"Build your first sentence","phase":"Present · essential verbs","goal":"Now, habits, and things generally true","why":"For a simple fact or routine, use present. Spanish usually omits yo when the ending identifies the speaker.","form":"For -ar verbs: drop -ar, add -o for yo (hablar → hablo). For -er: comer → como. For -ir: vivir → vivo.","en":"Every day I speak with a friend.","es":"Hablo con un amigo todos los días.","explanation":"hablar → hablo; the present tense fits a repeated habit.","wrong":"I am speaking yesterday.","wrongwhy":"Present describes now or a general habit, not a completed event yesterday.","verb":"hablar","level":"Start"},{"title":"Ser or estar?","phase":"Present · two kinds of “to be”","goal":"Identity vs location or state","why":"Use ser for identity, origin, and character; estar for location, condition, and ongoing action. Do not rely exclusively on permanent/temporary.","form":"soy / eres / es / somos / son; estoy / estás / está / estamos / están","en":"I am from New York, but I am tired today.","es":"Soy de Nueva York, pero hoy estoy cansado.","explanation":"Soy tells origin; estoy describes my current state.","wrong":"Estoy de Nueva York.","wrongwhy":"Origin is expressed with ser, not estar.","verb":"ser","level":"Start"},{"title":"Say what you need","phase":"Present · irregular high-frequency verbs","goal":"Needs, wants and ability","why":"The most useful daily verbs are often irregular: tengo, quiero, puedo, voy, hago. Use them as sentence engines.","form":"tener → tengo; querer → quiero; poder → puedo; hacer → hago","en":"I want to learn because I need to speak.","es":"Quiero aprender porque necesito hablar.","explanation":"Conjugate quiero; keep aprender and hablar in the infinitive after the other verbs.","wrong":"Quiero aprendo.","wrongwhy":"Two verbs together: conjugate the first and leave the second in infinitive.","verb":"querer","level":"Start"},{"title":"Questions and negatives","phase":"Present · word order","goal":"Ask for help and repair confusion","why":"A question can use the same verb order as a statement, with rising intonation and question punctuation. Put no before the conjugated verb.","form":"¿Puedes repetir? / No entiendo. / ¿Dónde está…?","en":"I do not understand. Can you repeat?","es":"No entiendo. ¿Puedes repetir?","explanation":"No comes immediately before entiendo; puedes + repetir uses an infinitive.","wrong":"No entiendo puedes repites.","wrongwhy":"The second verb stays in its infinitive after poder.","verb":"entender","level":"Start"},{"title":"Make a plan","phase":"Near future · ir a + infinitive","goal":"Intentions and immediate plans","why":"In everyday speech voy a + infinitive is often more natural than the formal future endings for plans.","form":"voy, vas, va, vamos, van + a + infinitive","en":"Tomorrow I am going to study Spanish.","es":"Mañana voy a estudiar español.","explanation":"Voy is the present form of ir; estudiar stays in the infinitive.","wrong":"Mañana voy estudiar español.","wrongwhy":"The a is required in ir a + infinitive.","verb":"ir","level":"Foundation"},{"title":"What is happening right now?","phase":"Present progressive","goal":"Something actively underway","why":"Use estar + -ando/-iendo when the action is in progress. Ordinary Spanish present already covers many English -ing meanings, so don’t overuse progressive.","form":"estoy hablando; estoy comiendo; estoy escribiendo","en":"I am eating right now.","es":"Estoy comiendo ahora mismo.","explanation":"Estar marks the current state; -iendo marks an action unfolding.","wrong":"Soy comiendo.","wrongwhy":"The progressive uses estar, not ser.","verb":"estar","level":"Foundation"},{"title":"Yesterday: a finished event","phase":"Preterite · -ar","goal":"An event with an endpoint","why":"Use the preterite to move a story forward: a finished action with a bounded time or clear completion.","form":"hablé, hablaste, habló, hablamos, hablaron","en":"Yesterday I spoke to the chef.","es":"Ayer hablé con el chef.","explanation":"Ayer points to a finished occasion, so the preterite hablé is natural.","wrong":"Ayer hablaba con el chef (for one completed call).","wrongwhy":"Imperfect could set background but by itself does not signal a single completed event.","verb":"hablar","level":"Everyday past"},{"title":"Yesterday: went, did, saw","phase":"Preterite · irregular","goal":"Five essential past-time verbs","why":"Several of the most useful past verbs change their stems entirely. Practice small stories rather than memorizing tables in isolation.","form":"ir/ser → fui, fue; hacer → hice; tener → tuve; ver → vi; estar → estuve","en":"Yesterday I went home and made dinner.","es":"Ayer fui a casa e hice la cena.","explanation":"Both fui and hice are completed events in sequence; e replaces y before the i sound in hice.","wrong":"Ayer iba a casa e hacía cena (as one finished chain).","wrongwhy":"The imperfect gives background or habitual actions rather than this completed sequence.","verb":"hacer","level":"Everyday past"},{"title":"How things used to be","phase":"Imperfect · routines/background","goal":"Repeated past and descriptions","why":"Use imperfect for the scene, past habits and ongoing conditions. The English clues “used to” or “was doing” often point here.","form":"hablaba, comía, vivía; irregular: era, iba, veía","en":"When I was a child, I lived near the park.","es":"Cuando era niño, vivía cerca del parque.","explanation":"Era and vivía describe a period and ongoing circumstances.","wrong":"Cuando fui niño, viví cerca del parque (as a general description).","wrongwhy":"For background or habitual states, imperfect is the default.","verb":"vivir","level":"Storytelling"},{"title":"Two past tenses, one story","phase":"Preterite vs imperfect","goal":"Interruptions and background","why":"Think film: the imperfect is the scene (rain falling); preterite advances the action (door opened). Both can appear in one sentence.","form":"Llovía cuando llegué. / Estaba leyendo cuando llamó.","en":"It was raining when I arrived.","es":"Llovía cuando llegué.","explanation":"Llovía sets the background; llegué is a completed arrival.","wrong":"Llovió cuando llegaba (to express the same meaning).","wrongwhy":"Past tense choice changes what is foregrounded; learn the event boundary.","verb":"llegar","level":"Storytelling"},{"title":"Use it, give it, show it","phase":"Object pronouns","goal":"Who receives what","why":"Direct object pronouns replace what is affected (lo/la); indirect objects mark to or for whom (le/les). Before one conjugated verb; attached to infinitives and positive commands.","form":"Lo veo. / Le doy el libro. / Quiero verlo.","en":"I see it and want to show it to her.","es":"Lo veo y quiero mostrárselo.","explanation":"Lo = the thing; se = to her (le changes to se before lo); mostrar + se + lo.","wrong":"Le lo quiero mostrar.","wrongwhy":"Before lo/la/los/las, le/les becomes se.","verb":"dar","level":"Communication"},{"title":"I like it vs it pleases me","phase":"Gustar and similar verbs","goal":"Preferences without English word order","why":"Gustar describes what is pleasing to someone. The thing liked controls gusta/gustan; the person is represented by me/te/le/nos/les.","form":"Me gusta el café. / Me gustan los libros.","en":"I like the books, but I prefer coffee.","es":"Me gustan los libros, pero prefiero el café.","explanation":"Libros is plural so use gustan; prefiero is an ordinary present verb.","wrong":"Me gusta los libros.","wrongwhy":"The verb agrees with the thing liked, not with me.","verb":"gustar","level":"Communication"},{"title":"Say what will happen","phase":"Simple future","goal":"Predictions, promises and confident statements","why":"Use infinitive + future endings for predictions, promises and certain forward-looking statements. For plans, ir a is often easier.","form":"hablaré, hablarás, hablará, hablaremos, hablarán; haré, tendré","en":"I will call you tomorrow.","es":"Te llamaré mañana.","explanation":"Llamaré is future first person; te identifies whom you will call.","wrong":"Te llamaré ayer.","wrongwhy":"A future event cannot ordinarily take yesterday as its time anchor.","verb":"llamar","level":"Beyond basic"},{"title":"Ask politely or imagine","phase":"Conditional","goal":"Would, could, hypothetical outcomes","why":"The conditional softens requests and describes what would happen under imagined conditions. Massie is especially good at describing the private court dramas through which large political decisions were made.","ideas":"01 Legitimacy must be built. Catherine was neither ethnically Russian nor the uncontested heir. She had to win the army, nobility, church and court through skillful performance and patronage. 02 Ideas collide with political constraints. Admiring Montesquieu and Voltaire did not mean emancipating serfs or dismantling autocracy. She balanced philosophical ambitions with the groups who kept her in power. 03 Expansion carries human costs. Military success and imperial prestige rested on war, coercion and governance over populations with little voice in their incorporation.","detail":"Catherine’s 1767 Legislative Commission, informed by her “Instruction,” made her Enlightenment interests visible. But deliberation did not translate into a fundamental restructuring of serfdom. Intellectual openness and governing incentives pulled in different directions. WHERE TO BE SKEPTICAL Massie’s biography is readable and sympathetic to Catherine; it is less centered on the lives of serfs, subject peoples and neighbors affected by imperial expansion. “Enlightened despot” is a useful tension, not a settled compliment. WHAT TO TAKE AWAY Her achievement was real, and so were her contradictions. The book is most valuable for seeing how a highly intelligent reformer can become the guardian of an unjust political structure."},{"title":"Foundation","author":"Isaac Asimov","category":"SCIENCE FICTION / POLITICAL HISTORY","brief":"The mathematician Hari Seldon uses “psychohistory,” a fictional statistical science of large populations, to foresee the Galactic Empire’s fall and a long dark age. His Foundation, established on remote Terminus, is ostensibly devoted to preserving knowledge. As the Empire retreats, the settlers survive a sequence of political crises not by superior military force but by adapting their leverage. Salvor Hardin uses diplomacy and religion to counter neighboring kingdoms; later, Hober Mallow turns commercial dependence into influence. The novel is a linked sequence of stories spanning generations, making institutions and historical change its true protagonists.","ideas":"01 Power depends on the era. When state authority fragments, knowledge, belief, trade and technical services become alternative sources of power. Yesterday’s winning institution may lose its advantage. 02 Large patterns are not individual scripts. Psychohistory works, in the fiction, on populations rather than perfectly predicting a single person. Asimov distinguishes historical forces from personal heroics. 03 A crisis can make hidden constraints visible. Each “Seldon Crisis” looks impossible until a changing balance of incentives suggests a path out of it.","detail":"Salvor Hardin discovers that Terminus can wield technical and religious authority without commanding the strongest army. Later the merchant Hober Mallow relies more on trade. Asimov changes the political weapon when the structure of the surrounding world changes. WHERE TO BE SKEPTICAL Psychohistory is a narrative invention, not an established science. The first novel’s style is talk-heavy, light on characterization and shaped by mid-twentieth-century assumptions. Its appeal is strategic thought, not psychological realism. WHAT TO TAKE AWAY The pleasure is watching political tools replace one another as circumstances change. It is a novel about institutions learning to survive a world larger than any one person."}].concat(V35_EXTRA_BOOKS);

function v35Save(key,independent=false){let p=v35Progress();p[key]={seen:true,mastered:!!independent||!!p[key]?.mastered,date:new Date().toISOString()};try{localStorage.setItem('studytable_v35_progress',JSON.stringify(p))}catch{}}
function v35Host(){let el=document.getElementById('v35Portal');if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='v35Portal';el.hidden=true;el.innerHTML='<div class="v35-shell" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Learn from the beginning"><div id="v35Content"></div></div>';document.body.append(el)}return el;}
function v35Open(section='home',index=0){if(typeof v34Close==='function'&&v34State)v34Close();v35={section,index,step:0,help:false,errors:0,revealed:false,input:'',filter:'',independent:false,verbQuery:'',mode:'grammar'};v35Host().hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('v35-active');v35Render();}
function v35Close(){v35Host().hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('v35-active');}

function v35Navigator(section,arr){return `<div class="v35-progress"><label>Lesson <select id="v35Select">${arr.map((x,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${i===v35.index?'selected':''}>${i+1}. ${v35h(x.title)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><span>${v35Count(section,arr.length)} / ${arr.length} explored</span></div>`}

function v35Footer(section,arr){return `<div class="v35-foot"><button data-v35-prev ${v35.index===0?'disabled':''}>← Previous lesson</button><button data-v35-next>${v35.index===arr.length-1?'Start over':'Next lesson →'}</button></div>`}
function v35WireFooter(section,arr){document.querySelector('[data-v35-prev]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>v35Open(section,Math.max(0,v35.index-1)));document.querySelector('[data-v35-next]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>v35Open(section,(v35.index+1)%arr.length));}
function v35RealKeyboard(){
 const white=[];for(const octave of [4,5])for(const note of ['C','D','E','F','G','A','B'])white.push(note+octave);white.push('C6');
 const after={C:'C#',D:'D#',F:'F#',G:'G#',A:'A#'};
 return `<div class="v35-real-keyboard" role="group" aria-label="Piano: white and black keys from middle C to high C" style="--v35-white-count:${white.length}">
  ${white.map((n,i)=>`<button type="button" class="v35-white-key" data-v34-key="${n}" aria-label="Play ${n}"><span>${n}</span></button>`).join('')}
  ${white.map((n,i)=>after[n[0]]&&i<14?`<button type="button" class="v35-black-key" data-v34-key="${after[n[0]]+n.slice(1)}" aria-label="Play ${after[n[0]]+n.slice(1)}" style="left:${(i+1)*100/white.length}%"><span>${after[n[0]]+n.slice(1)}</span></button>`:'').join('')}
 </div>`;
}
function v35Piano(){const d=V35_PIANO[v35.index],target=d.notes[v35.step],completed=v35.step>=d.notes.length;let kb=v35RealKeyboard();v35Frame(`${v35Navigator('piano',V35_PIANO)}<div class="v35-lesson"><span class="v35-eyebrow">PIANO · ${v35.index+1} / ${V35_PIANO.length} · NO TIMER</span><h3>${v35h(d.title)}</h3><div class="v35-explain"><strong>Understand first</strong><p>${v35h(d.why)}</p><p><b>Your fingers:</b> ${v35h(d.finger)}</p></div><div class="v35-task"><span>WHAT TO DO RIGHT NOW</span><h4>${v35h(d.prompt)}</h4>${!completed?`<p>Your next note: <b class="v35-big-note" id="v35Target">${v35.independent?'Find it by memory':v35h(target)}</b> · Note ${v35.step+1} of ${d.notes.length}</p>`:'<p class="v35-success">You played the entire sequence. Great — you can replay it or continue.</p>'}<p class="v35-key-help">C4 (middle C) is immediately to the left of a pair of black keys. On this virtual keyboard it is the far-left white key.</p></div><div class="v35-piano-bar"><label><input type="checkbox" id="v35Memory" ${v35.independent?'checked':''}/> Play from memory (hide the target note)</label><button id="v35ShowKeys">${v35.independent?'Reveal note':'Show / play the answer'}</button><button id="v35Replay">Start sequence again</button></div><div class="v35-piano" id="v35Keyboard">${kb}</div><div id="v35PianoFeedback" class="v35-feedback" role="status">${completed?'You finished. Try again from memory to check recall.':'Press the named key. There is no time limit.'}</div><p class="v35-key-tip">Optional computer keyboard: A S D F G H J = C D E F G A B (the first seven white keys). You can also click or tap.</p></div>${v35Footer('piano',V35_PIANO)}`);v35WireNav();v35WireFooter('piano',V35_PIANO);const paint=()=>{document.querySelectorAll('#v35Keyboard [data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('v35-target',!v35.independent&&b.dataset.v34Key===d.notes[v35.step]));const e=document.getElementById('v35Target');if(e)e.textContent=v35.independent?'Find it by memory':d.notes[v35.step]||'Complete'};paint();document.querySelectorAll('#v35Keyboard [data-v34-key]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>v35PianoPress(b.dataset.v34Key));document.getElementById('v35Memory').onchange=e=>{v35.independent=e.target.checked;paint();document.getElementById('v35PianoFeedback').textContent=v35.independent?'Try it without the note highlight. '+d.why;const b=document.querySelector('#v35Keyboard [data-v34-key="'+n+'"]');if(b){b.classList.add('v35-flash');setTimeout(()=>b.classList.remove('v35-flash'),750)}};document.getElementById('v35Replay').onclick=()=>{v35.step=0;v35.help=false;v35.errors=0;v35Piano()};}
function v35PianoPress(n){const d=V35_PIANO[v35.index];v27Sound(n);if(v35.step>=d.notes.length)return;const needed=d.notes[v35.step];const feedback=document.getElementById('v35PianoFeedback');if(n!==needed){v35.errors++;feedback.textContent='That was '+n+'. Look for '+needed+'. '+(v35.independent?'Turn off memory mode if you need a hint.': 'Follow the highlighted key.');return;}v35.step++;if(v35.step===d.notes.length){v35Save('piano:'+v35.index,v35.independent&&!v35.help&&!v35.errors);v35Piano();return;}feedback.textContent='Yes — that was '+n+'.
